Two changes to the 2am-crying selfie video renderer in /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop (git repo; the renderer engine is shared/renderer/, the song's scenes are songs/2am-crying/video/; read songs/2am-crying/video/README.md and songs/2am-crying/TREATMENT.md first). Don't touch songs/2am-crying/synth/, songs/baby-facts/, or shared/suno/. You can view PNGs with the Read tool; use that to iterate visually (--stills and --sheet in render.mjs).

1. Bug: real-vocal timing drops the section ids, so the bridge, the final grid and the outro loop never fire

The video now runs on a real Suno vocal. Its timing lives in songs/2am-crying/video/data/lyrics.json and audio.json, produced by shared/align/align.py + analyze.py from songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/it-s-2am-and-i-m-crying-bright-13e92be2.mp3 and the sheet songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt (the mock timing it replaced is kept in songs/2am-crying/video/data-mock/). The aligned lyrics.json lines have keys i, text, start, end, words but no section, while clips.js and story.js select clips by line.section with the ids intro, chorus1, verse1, chorus2, verse2, bridge, final, outro. Result in the current draft (songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-suno-E2-540p.mp4): no bridge long take (so no bottle during the milk verse, which the user noticed), no final-chorus grid, and the outro hello loop clip probably didn't match either. audio.json.sections from analyze.py are generic s1..s11, so they can't be used for this.

Fix it at both ends:

  • In shared/align/align.py (and common.py where the sheet is parsed), emit section on every line of lyrics.json, derived from the sheet's [Section - notes] headers with this naming: take the text before - , lowercase, drop spaces (Verse 1 → verse1), Final Chorus → final, and number repeated plain names by occurrence (Chorus, Chorus → chorus1, chorus2). Keep bv as is. Re-run the alignment for the runE2 clip (it is cached in shared/align/work/, so it takes seconds: uv run --project /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align python /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/align/align.py --audio <mp3> --lyrics <sheet> --out /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/data) and copy the new lyrics.json into songs/2am-crying/video/data/ (keep the source and lyrics_sheet fields that are already in that audio.json). Also update shared/align/README.md and EVAL.md only where they describe the schema.
  • In the renderer, make clips.js/story.js robust: if lines have no section, derive it from the sheet named in audio.json.lyrics_sheet (same naming), and log a warning. Also log at render start which plates were created from sections (bridge long take, final chorus grid, outro loop) so this silent failure can't recur.
  • Then confirm with stills that the bridge long take shows the bottle during "10:50 … 10:58 and I'm heavy", that the final chorus is the filling grid, and that the outro frame equals the intro frame (--check does the loop test).

Note: shared/renderer/core/timeline.js was just patched to resolve two clauses snapping to the same downbeat (real vocals aren't on the grid); keep that.

2. Redesign the character as baby Clawd

The user's note: "the baby is... not that cute? have we considered baby clawd?" Clawd is the Claude Code mascot and the protagonist of the earlier "I'm Upping My P(doom)" video, so a baby Clawd is the right lead for a "clawdpop" video. Reference model sheets (view them with Read): /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad/clawd_ref/views.jpg and .../emotions.jpg, plus the p5 source .../clawd.js (its comments document the geometry: body 10 units wide by 6 tall with slightly rounded corners, four stubby legs under it, two little arm nubs at the sides, eyes at (±2.5u, −6u) each 1u wide and 2u tall as dark rounded rectangles with a white highlight dot, mouth near (0, −4.3u), body colour terracotta clay around #D77757 with a darker band low on the body, dark ink outline). These are reference only; do not copy files into the repo; draw it fresh in Canvas 2D in our flat style.

Baby-fy it: chubbier, nearly square body with rounder corners; legs shorter and stubbier; eyes bigger and set lower with a bigger highlight; a tuft or single curl on top; blush; a pacifier for the quiet and sleeping moments (a round shield with a ring over the mouth position); the night outfit is a sleep sack that wraps the legs (a soft bag with a zipper line), the day outfit a onesie band across the lower body with two snaps; "naked" after the bath is the bare clay body; "towel" is a cape. One arm nub always extends toward the bottom corner holding the phone (we see the nub stretched, not the phone), as now. Expressions map onto the existing API: eyes open/half/closed/squeeze/wide/focus → eyelids and squints on the rectangular Clawd eyes; mouth line/o/smile/grin/press → Clawd's small mouth (keep the crying O; the pooping "press" is a flat line with a furrowed brow); tears, blush, flash, stain (milk on the body), look (pupil highlight shifts) must all still work. The crying face should still read as crying from across a room.

Keep drawBaby(ctx, o) and BABY_DEFAULTS as the API so every scene keeps working; change the drawing in baby.js (and the character's colours in the palette). Check every clip type on the contact sheet: crib (night, flash), standing in the crib, high chair with food, closeup with bottle and book, bath with droplets, naked towel run, nap and the bridge long take with the bottle sliding off, pooping hold, rear-camera parent (the parent can stay human or become an adult Clawd asleep in the chair; adult Clawd is the better joke), grid. The sleeping-parent silhouette in the doorway and on the floor during 2am/3am/4am should match whatever the parent becomes.

1	# 2AM (And I'm Crying): the video
2	
3	Treatment v3, "the baby's selfie video" (`../TREATMENT.md`), over the v3 lyric sheet
4	(`../suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`). Every frame is a pure function of song time, painted on a
5	canvas in headless Chrome and encoded with ffmpeg. The engine is song-agnostic and lives in
6	`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby, props, the caption
7	and status-bar styling, the time-of-day grade and the edit rules.
8	
9	Review surface: `sheets/plates.png` (one cell per clip, 9:16) and `sheets/layout-16x9.png`.
10	
11	## Commands
12	
13	All from the repo root (`/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop`). One-time setup:
14	
15	```sh
16	npm install --prefix shared/renderer          # puppeteer-core only; drives the installed Chrome
17	```
18	
19	Preview (scrub, play against the audio; space, arrows = beat, shift+arrows = bar, `[` `]` = clip):
20	
21	```sh
22	node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs        # then open:
23	# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=23
24	# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?layout=16x9&t=40
25	```
26	
27	Draft render (9:16, 540x960, 30 fps, audio picked from `data/audio.json` "source"):
28	
29	```sh
30	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video \
31	  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-540p.mp4 --check
32	# 16:9 draft: add --layout 16x9 (960x540) and a different --out
33	```
34	
35	Final renders (1080p60):
36	
37	```sh
38	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --w 1080 --h 1920 --fps 60 \
39	  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1080x1920-60.mp4
40	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --layout 16x9 --w 1920 --h 1080 --fps 60 \
41	  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1920x1080-60.mp4
42	```
43	
44	Contact sheets and stills:
45	
46	```sh
47	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/plates.png
48	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --layout 16x9 \
49	  --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/layout-16x9.png --sheet-mode even --count 12 --cols 4 --cell-w 480
50	node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --stills 4.5,40,77
51	node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --q t=23 --q "layout=16x9&t=40"   # preview page loads cleanly
52	```
53	
54	Timing and placeholder audio:
55	
56	```sh
57	node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs     # regenerate data/lyrics.json + data/audio.json
58	node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs --plan   # ignore the synth, use the 128 BPM plan
59	node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs      # audio/click.wav (fallback audio, gitignored)
60	```
61	
62	`render.mjs` options: `--from/--to` seconds, `--workers N` (default ~60% of cores),
63	`--crf 16`, `--preset medium`, `--audio file`, `--force` (allow audio/timing length mismatch),
64	`--check` (determinism: ordered vs shuffled frames across workers; loop: outro frames equal
65	intro frames). Video is libx264 CRF 16, yuv420p, tagged BT.709; audio AAC 192k.
66	
67	## Data
68	
69	- `data/lyrics.json`: `{ lines: [{ i, text, start, end, section, words: [{ w, start, end, conf, syl? }] }], extras, notes }`
70	  (mexicat/pdoom-video schema; `section` is a convenience, the engine recomputes it from audio.json).
71	- `data/audio.json`: `{ bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{ name, start, end }], kicks, duration }` plus
72	  added optional fields: `beats_per_bar`, `hats` (offbeat onsets), `quiet` (silent spans: the bar
73	  after "1am and I'm..."), `risers`, `source` ("plan" or "synth (...)": render.mjs and the preview
74	  pick the audio file from it), `lyrics_sheet`.
75	
76	`tools/mock_timing.mjs` builds both. It prefers `../synth/out/song.json` when that arrangement
77	fits the lyric sheet (same section order, at least a bar per line); it then takes the beat grid,
78	sections and kicks from the synth and times every word from the synth's sung syllables
79	(`lyric_lines`, `adlibs`). Otherwise it lays the sheet out on the plan: intro 2, chorus 8,
80	verse1 8, chorus 8, verse2 9 (8 + the bar of silence after "1am and I'm..."), bridge 8, final
81	chorus 12, outro 2 = 57 bars at 128 BPM (106.875 s), clauses sharing each line, words spread
82	evenly over 90% of their span. Current source: **synth** (iteration 7 matches the plan bar for
83	bar). When the Suno vocal is aligned (`shared/align`), drop its `lyrics.json`/`audio.json` in
84	`data/`: clips are derived from lyric content, so nothing else changes. Watch the console for
85	`[clips] ... matched no rule` (a clause whose text drifted falls back to the closeup clip).
86	
87	Audio: the draft uses `../synth/out/2am_crying.wav` when `audio.json.source` is the synth
88	(the full-song file, chosen by duration); otherwise `audio/click.wav`, a click + kick + hats +
89	riser track generated from audio.json by `tools/make_click.mjs` (written in Node, no ffmpeg
90	filters needed). render.mjs refuses audio whose length differs from audio.json by more than a bar.
91	
92	## How it is put together
93	
94	- `clips.js`: splits every lyric line into time clauses ("It's <time> ..."), maps each clause's
95	  text to a clip type + params (the rule table), and returns the timeline: one window per
96	  clause (cut on the downbeat; on the half-bar in the double-time verses, so two cuts per bar),
97	  one long-take window for the bridge, one grid window for the final chorus, and the outro.
98	- `story.js`: status-bar clock (the clause's time; minute by minute in the long take), battery
99	  (drains, charges with a bolt during naps, ends the day in the red), record timer, and the
100	  loop: outro frames are intro frames, quantized to the frame grid (`--check` verifies it).
101	- `song.js`: the compositor. Clip under the camera transform (handshake + a slight kick shake +
102	  per-clip tilt, owned here so a clip can be held still) -> lens post (grain on a fixed 24 Hz
103	  tick, vignette) -> lens overlays (splat, droplets, finger) -> caption -> status bar; then the
104	  layout: full frame (9:16) or the phone centred on a soft nursery backdrop with a sun/moon
105	  arc (16:9).
106	- `tod.js`: time-of-day light (deep blue night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm,
107	  blue 8pm), the night flash palette, the phone-screen glow.
108	- `baby.js`, `props.js`, `monitor.js` (the v1 IR monitor view, cut into 7:45 "sleeping").
109	- `scenes/`: one module per clip type: `hello`, `crib`, `nap`, `pooping`, `closeup`, `highchair`,
110	  `standing`, `fan`, `bath`, `rear`, `grid`.
111

145	## Deliberate departures
146	
147	- Verse 2 is 9 bars (the brief's 8 plus the bar of silence after "1am and I'm..."), 57 bars total.
148	- No wide-angle edge stretch: it needs a per-frame offscreen remap for an effect that is barely
149	  visible at phone size. Day "over-saturation" is carried by the time-of-day grade instead.
150	- The 12th grid tile (11 final-chorus lines in a 3x4 grid) is the record button rather than a clip.
151	- The drinking clips hold the bottle with one hand: the other hand is always on the phone.

Readsongs/2am-crying/TREATMENT.md
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/TREATMENT.md
result
1	# Treatment v3: the baby's selfie video
2	
3	Supersedes `TREATMENT-v1-monitor.md` (the nursery-camera concept; still a good source for
4	the night clips and the baby character). Lyrics: `LYRICS.md` → "v3: the day", and
5	`suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`.
6	
7	## Concept
8	
9	The baby is filming a day-in-my-life video on a phone, front camera, in the style of the
10	"Imma Be" selfie trend: one clip per timestamp, a caption in the corner that reads exactly
11	what the lyric says, hard cuts on the beat, and the day looping back to 2am at the end.
12	Every line of the song is "It's [time] and I'm [doing]", so every line is one clip and the
13	caption is the lyric. The clock is still the escalating number, but now it lives in the
14	phone's status bar and in the caption.
15	
16	Tone: the baby is sincere. It is not performing misery; it is documenting. The phone is
17	held badly (too close, tilted, thumb on the lens once), which is where the charm comes from.
18	
19	## Formats
20	
21	- Primary: vertical 1080x1920 (9:16), the trend's native shape. 60 fps final, 30 fps drafts.
22	- Secondary: 1920x1080 (16:9) with the vertical phone frame centered on a soft nursery
23	  backdrop (the room out of focus, time-of-day lighting), for YouTube. Same scenes, one
24	  layout switch.
25	
26	## Style bible
27	
28	- **Look.** A phone front camera: slight wide-angle stretch at the edges, mild over-
29	  saturation by day, grain and a hard flash at night (the flash lights the baby's face and
30	  nothing else). Not the IR greyscale of v1, except as a joke for the 7:45pm "sleeping" clip,
31	  where the parents' monitor view is cut in.
32	- **Caption.** The trend style: lowercase, heavy grotesque (Archivo Black / Inter 900),
33	  white with a black outline or on a rounded black pill, upper third of the frame. The
34	  caption is the lyric line, revealed word by word in sync, and stays until the cut. Times
35	  are digits ("it's 2am and i'm crying").
36	- **Status bar.** A thin phone status bar at the top: the clock (which is the escalation
37	  number), battery draining across the day and charging during naps, a red record dot
38	  with a running timer.
39	- **Baby.** The same procedural character as v1: round head, two dot eyes, a few hairs,
40	  stubby arms; sleep sack at night, onesie by day. One arm always extends toward the bottom
41	  corner of the frame, holding the phone (we see the upper arm and the elbow, not the phone).
42	  Expressions are tiny: eyes open/closed/half, mouth a line, an O, or a small smile.
43	- **Time of day.** Background colour temperature and brightness follow the clock: deep blue
44	  night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm, blue again at 8pm. A tiny sun/moon
45	  arc in the backdrop of the 16:9 layout.
46	- **Motion.** Cuts on downbeats. In the double-time verses, two cuts per bar. The phone
47	  shakes with the kick, slightly. Slams on "pooping again". No cute wobble beyond the
48	  selfie handshake.
49	- **Bans.** No photos, no real faces, no emoji-style faces, no diaper-contents humour.
50	  "Pooping" is a face and a caption, nothing else.
51	
52	## Clips
53	
54	Each clip is anchored to its lyric line by content. Duration is whatever the line's words
55	cover, snapped to the beat grid.
56	
57	| line | clip |
58	|---|---|
59	| intro "Hi. It's me." | Phone wakes; the baby's face too close, then pulls back; a wave; record dot starts. |
60	| 2am crying | Dark crib, flash on, mouth an O, eyes squeezed; the caption's "crying" slams. |
61	| 3am crying | Same framing, phone tilted 15°, a parent silhouette appearing in the doorway behind. |
62	| 4am crying | Same framing, the parent silhouette now sitting on the floor next to the crib. |
63	| 11am napping | Hard cut to bright daylight, eyes closed, the phone slowly tipping over to the ceiling fan. |
64	| 12pm crying | High chair, daylight, a spoon mid-air. |
65	| 1pm pooping | Concentration face. Caption only. Hold absolutely still. |
66	| 2pm pooping again | Identical frame to 1pm; the caption gets "again" slammed on in red. |
67	| 5am quiet | Nearly black; just the record dot and two open eyes. |
68	| 5:45 up | Flash; the baby standing, grinning; the parents' alarm clock visible behind: 5:45. |
69	| 6am standing / 6:15 still standing | Same frame twice, the second with a timer overlay. |
70	| 7am drinking / 7:30 wearing it | Bottle with both hands, eyes half closed; then a milk-stained onesie, proud. |
71	| 8am eating / 8:05 throwing it | High chair; then food arcing past the lens, a splat on the glass. |

73	| 10am pointing / 10:30 pointing at the fan | Finger into the lens; then camera whips up to the ceiling fan. |
74	| 10:45 fighting it | Eyes closing and snapping open, twice, on the beat. |
75	| 3pm napping | Daylight nap in a car seat or stroller, head at an angle. |
76	| 4pm drinking | Bottle, golden light. |
77	| 5pm crying / 5:30 crying at the fan | Witching hour; then the fan again, accusingly. |
78	| 6pm eating / 6:05 the floor is eating | High chair; then camera tilts down: food on the floor, caption deadpan. |
79	| 7pm splashing | Bath; water droplets on the lens; the caption drips. |
80	| 7:15 naked and running | Motion blur, towel cape, the frame shaking, laughter. |
81	| 7:30 reading / 7:45 "sleeping" | Book in the crib; then dark room, eyes wide open, caption with quotation marks; a one-second cut-in of the v1 IR monitor view. |
82	| 8pm, 9pm sleeping | Dark, eyes closed, the phone drifting. |
83	| 10pm you're sleeping | Camera flips to the rear: a parent asleep in the chair, mouth open. The only rear-camera shot. |
84	| 11pm we're sleeping / 12am sleeping | Darker, slower, the record timer the only motion. |
85	| 1am and I'm... | Black. Record dot. One bar of silence. Eyes open. |
86	| bridge 10:50 → 11am | The one long take: bottle, eyes heavy, the phone tilting degree by degree until it lies flat looking at the ceiling fan; the fan's blades slow with the half-time. |
87	| final chorus | The day as a filling grid of all the clips, 3 wide, each tile lighting on its line, until the 1am tile goes black. |
88	| outro "Hi. It's me." | The intro clip again, frame-exact, so the video loops. |

90	## Technical notes
91	
92	Same engine contract as before: deterministic frames, `data/lyrics.json` word timings,
93	`data/audio.json` beat grid and sections. One scene module per clip type (crib-night,
94	high-chair, bath, standing, nap, pooping-face, fan, rear-camera, grid), parameterised by
95	time-of-day and caption, instead of one module per lyric line. The caption component and
96	the status bar are shared. A `layout` switch selects 9:16 or 16:9.

// Clips from lyric content. Every "It's <time> and I'm <doing>" clause is one clip; the
// rule table maps the clause's words to a clip TYPE (scene module) and its params. The
// bridge is one long take, the final chorus one grid, the outro is the intro again.

import { norm } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js';

const HM = (h, m = 0) => h * 60 + m;

/** "2am" / "12pm" / "5:45" / "10:30" -> minutes since midnight. am/pm inferred for h:mm. */
export function minutesOf(text, prevMin = null) {
  const s = String(text).toLowerCase();
  let m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*([ap])\.?\s*m\b/);
  if (m) { let h = +m[1] % 12; if (m[2] === 'p') h += 12; return HM(h); }
  m = s.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  if (m) {
    // h:mm without am/pm: take the reading closest after the previous clause's time
    const h = +m[1] % 12, mm = +m[2];
    const am = HM(h, mm), pm = HM(h + 12, mm);
    if (prevMin == null) return am;
    const fwd = (x) => (x - prevMin + 1440) % 1440;
    return fwd(am) <= fwd(pm) ? am : pm;
  }
  return null;
}

/** Split lyric lines into time clauses: a clause starts at "It's" followed by a word with a digit. */
export function clausesOf(L) {
  const out = [];
  let prevMin = null;
  for (const line of L.lines) {
    let cur = null;
    line.words.forEach((w, k) => {
      const next = line.words[k + 1];
      const starts = norm(w.w) === 'its' && next && /\d/.test(next.w) && !w.backing;
      if (starts) {
        cur = { words: [], line, section: line.section, start: w.start };
        out.push(cur);
      }
      if (cur) cur.words.push(w);
      else if (out.length && out.at(-1).section === line.section && !/^(intro|outro)$/.test(line.section)) out.at(-1).words.push(w);
    });
  }
  for (const c of out) {
    c.text = c.words.filter((w) => !w.backing).map((w) => w.w).join(' ');
    c.n = norm(c.text);
    c.end = c.words.at(-1).end;
    c.min = minutesOf(c.text, prevMin);
    prevMin = c.min;
    c.verb = c.n.replace(/^its \S+ (and )?/, '');
  }
  out.forEach((c, i) => { c.index = i; });
  return out;
}

// [pattern on the normalized clause, type, params]. First match wins.
export const RULES = [
  [/^its 2am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 0 }],
  [/^its 3am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 1 }],
  [/^its 4am and im crying/, 'crib', { stage: 2 }],
  [/^its 5am and im quiet/, 'crib', { mode: 'quiet' }],
  [/^its 7:45 and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'fake' }],
  [/^its (8pm|9pm) and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep' }],
  [/^its 11pm and were sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep', dark: 1 }],
  [/^its 12am and im sleeping/, 'crib', { mode: 'sleep', dark: 2 }],
  [/^its 1am and im/, 'crib', { mode: 'void' }],
  [/^its 10pm and youre sleeping/, 'rear', {}],
  [/^its 11am and im napping/, 'nap', { mode: 'tip' }],
  [/^its 3pm and im napping/, 'nap', { mode: 'carseat' }],
  [/^its 1pm and im pooping$/, 'pooping', {}],
  [/^its 2pm and im pooping again/, 'pooping', { again: true }],
  [/^its 12pm and im crying/, 'highchair', { mode: 'spoon' }],
  [/^its (8am|6pm) and im eating/, 'highchair', { mode: 'eat' }],
  [/^its 8:05 and im throwing it/, 'highchair', { mode: 'throw' }],
  [/^its 6:05 and the floor is eating/, 'highchair', { mode: 'floor' }],
  [/^its 5:45 and im up/, 'standing', { mode: 'up' }],
  [/^its 6am and im standing/, 'standing', { mode: 'stand' }],
  [/^its 6:15 and im still standing/, 'standing', { mode: 'stand', timer: true }],
  [/^its 7:30 and im wearing it/, 'standing', { mode: 'wearing' }],
  [/^its 7:15 and im naked and im running/, 'standing', { mode: 'run' }],
  [/^its 7pm and im splashing/, 'bath', {}],
  [/^its 10:30 and im pointing at the fan/, 'fan', { mode: 'point' }],
  [/^its 5:30 and im crying at the fan/, 'fan', { mode: 'cry' }],
  [/^its (7am|4pm) and im drinking/, 'closeup', { prop: 'bottle' }],
  [/^its 9am and im reading/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book' }],
  [/^its 9:01 and im eating it/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book', bite: true }],
  [/^its 7:30 and im reading/, 'closeup', { prop: 'book', crib: true }],
  [/^its 10am and im pointing/, 'closeup', { prop: 'point' }],
  [/^its 10:45 and im fighting it/, 'closeup', { mode: 'fight' }],
  [/^its 5pm and im crying/, 'closeup', { mode: 'cry' }],
];

export function ruleFor(clause) {
  for (const [re, type, params] of RULES) if (re.test(clause.n)) return { type, params: { ...params }, matched: String(re) };
  return { type: 'closeup', params: {}, matched: null };
}

/** Timeline spec function: the engine calls it with the parsed lyrics and beat grid. */
export function timeline({ lyrics, beats }) {
  const clauses = clausesOf(lyrics);
  const entries = [{ plate: 'hello', at: { time: 0 }, params: { loop: false }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 }];
  const hero80 = (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.8;
  const fallbacks = [];
  for (const c of clauses) {
    if (c.section === 'bridge') {
      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'nap' && e.params.mode === 'long')) {
        entries.push({ plate: 'nap', params: { mode: 'long', clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'bridge').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'bridge long take' }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.5 });
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (c.section === 'final') {
      if (!entries.some((e) => e.plate === 'grid')) {
        entries.push({ plate: 'grid', params: { clauses: clauses.filter((x) => x.section === 'final').map((x) => x.index) }, at: { t: c.start, why: 'final chorus grid' }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.55 });
      }
      continue;
    }
    const rule = ruleFor(c);
    if (!rule.matched) fallbacks.push(c.text);
    entries.push({
      plate: rule.type,
      params: { ...rule.params, clause: c.index },
      at: { t: c.start, why: c.text },
      snap: /^verse/.test(c.section) ? 'halfbar' : 'downbeat',
      hero: hero80,
    });
  }
  const outro = lyrics.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
  if (outro) entries.push({ plate: 'hello', params: { loop: true }, at: { line: 'Hi', section: 'outro' }, hero: (w) => w.start + w.dur * 0.62 });
  if (fallbacks.length) console.warn(`[clips] ${fallbacks.length} clause(s) matched no rule and use the closeup fallback:\n  ${fallbacks.join('\n  ')}`);
  return entries;
}
// Song-level curves for v3 "the day", all derived from lyric content:
//   loopTime(t)  outro frames map frame-exactly onto intro frames (the video loops)
//   clock(t)     status-bar clock, minutes since midnight (the escalating number)
//   battery(t)   drains across the day, charges during naps
//   rec(t)       record dot + running timer (starts with the beat in the intro)
//   clause(t)    the time clause on screen (caption words, minutes)

import { clamp, lerp, bisect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { clausesOf } from './clips.js';

const NAP_TYPES = new Set(['nap']);

export function makeStory(env) {
  const { L, B, T } = env;
  const clauses = clausesOf(L);
  const W = T.windows;
  const intro = W[0];
  const outro = W.find((w) => w.plate === 'hello' && w.params.loop) || null;

  // The outro is the intro again, frame-exactly: quantize to the frame grid so outro frame
  // k shows intro frame j (same t -> same pixels, every input included).
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!outro || t < outro.start) return t;
    const fps = env.fps || 30;
    return intro.start + Math.round((t - outro.start) * fps) / fps;
  }
  const windowFor = (t) => (outro && t >= outro.start ? intro : T.windowAt(t));

  // ---- which clause is on screen ---------------------------------------------------
  function clauseAt(t, w = windowFor(t)) {
    const p = w.params;
    if (p.clause != null) return clauses[p.clause];
    if (p.clauses) {
      const list = p.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
      let cur = list[0];
      for (const c of list) if (B.snap(c.start, 'downbeat') <= t + 1e-6 || c.start <= t) cur = c;
      return cur;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // ---- clock -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const INTRO_MIN = 119; // 1:59am: the phone wakes a minute before the first clip
  function clock(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    if (w.plate === 'hello') return INTRO_MIN;
    if (w.plate === 'nap' && w.params.mode === 'long') {
      // the one long take ticks minute by minute between the sung times
      const list = w.params.clauses.map((i) => clauses[i]);
      const i = bisect(list, t, (c) => c.start);
      if (i < 0) return list[0].min;
      const a = list[i], b = list[i + 1];
      if (!b) return a.min + Math.floor(clamp((t - a.start) / (w.end - a.start)) * 1.99);
      return Math.floor(lerp(a.min, b.min, clamp((t - a.start) / (b.start - a.start))));
    }
    const c = clauseAt(t, w);
    return c ? c.min : INTRO_MIN;
  }

  // ---- battery: integrate drain / charge over the windows ------------------------------
  const DRAIN = 1.8, CHARGE = 2.0; // percent per second: ends the day in the red
  const isNap = (w) => NAP_TYPES.has(w.plate);
  const batt0 = [];
  let lvl = 100;
  for (const w of W) {
    batt0.push(lvl);
    if (w === outro) break;
    lvl = clamp(lvl + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * w.dur, 4, 100);
  }
  function battery(t) {
    const w = windowFor(t);
    const i = W.indexOf(w);
    const v = batt0[i] + (isNap(w) ? CHARGE : -DRAIN) * (t - w.start);
    return { level: clamp(v, 4, 100) / 100, charging: isNap(w) };
  }

  // ---- record dot + timer -------------------------------------------------------------
  const untz = L.findLine({ match: 'untz', section: 'intro' });
  const recStart = untz ? B.snap(untz.start, 'downbeat') : intro.start + B.barDur;
  function rec(t) {
    const tt = loopTime(t);
    if (tt < recStart) return { on: false, secs: 0 };
    return { on: true, secs: tt - recStart };
  }

  return { clauses, loopTime, windowFor, clauseAt, clock, battery, rec, recStart, intro, outro };
}
BashRead engine lyrics, timeline and boot modules6:15 AM

cat /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/core/timeline.js /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/core/boot.js
result
// Lyric helpers over data/lyrics.json (lines -> words -> {w, start, end, conf, syl}).
// Matching is case- and punctuation-insensitive so anchors survive the real alignment.

import { bisect } from './util.js';

/** Normalize for matching: lowercase, a.m. -> am, drop punctuation, collapse spaces. */
export function norm(s) {
  return String(s)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[‘’“”]/g, "'")
    .replace(/\b([ap])\.\s?m\.?/g, '$1m')
    .replace(/(\d)\s+([ap]m)\b/g, '$1$2')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9:' ]+/g, ' ')
    .replace(/'/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

export function makeLyrics(json, beats) {
  const lines = (json.lines || []).map((l, k) => {
    const words = (l.words && l.words.length ? l.words : fakeWords(l)).map((w, j) => ({ ...w, j }));
    return { ...l, i: l.i ?? k, words, n: norm(l.text) };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  lines.forEach((l, k) => { l.k = k; });
  // Section membership from audio.json (robust to alignment output without a section field).
  if (beats) {
    for (const l of lines) {
      const probe = l.start + Math.min(beats.beatDur, 0.3 * (l.end - l.start));
      l.section = beats.sectionAt(probe)?.name ?? l.section;
    }
  }
  // Backing-vocal flags: words inside parentheses.
  for (const l of lines) {
    let depth = 0;
    for (const w of l.words) {
      const opens = (w.w.match(/\(/g) || []).length, closes = (w.w.match(/\)/g) || []).length;
      w.backing = depth > 0 || opens > 0;
      depth += opens - closes;
    }
  }
  const allWords = lines.flatMap((l) => l.words.map((w) => ({ ...w, line: l })));

  function findLine({ match = null, section = null, nth = 0, after = null } = {}) {
    const m = match ? norm(match) : null;
    const hits = lines.filter((l) => (!section || l.section === section) && (!m || l.n.includes(m)) && (after == null || l.start >= after));
    return hits[nth] || null;
  }
  function lineAt(t) {
    const i = bisect(lines, t, (l) => l.start);
    return i >= 0 && t < lines[i].end ? lines[i] : null;
  }
  /**
   * The line the karaoke band should show at t: the most recent line that has started
   * (or starts within `lead`), held until the next one takes over or `hold` after its end.
   */
  function displayLine(t, lead = 0.25, hold = 0.6) {
    const i = bisect(lines, t + lead, (l) => l.start);
    if (i < 0) return null;
    const l = lines[i];
    if (t > l.end + hold) return null;
    return l;
  }
  function wordAt(t) {
    const l = lineAt(t);
    if (!l) return null;
    const j = bisect(l.words, t, (w) => w.start);
    return j >= 0 ? l.words[j] : null;
  }
  /** Find a word in a line by normalized text; `loose` falls back to the first word with digits. */
  function findWord(line, match, { nth = 0, loose = false } = {}) {
    if (!line) return null;
    const m = norm(match);
    const hits = line.words.filter((w) => norm(w.w) === m || norm(w.w).includes(m));
    if (hits[nth]) return hits[nth];
    if (loose) {
      const digits = line.words.filter((w) => /\d/.test(w.w));
      if (digits.length) return digits[0];
    }
    return null;
  }
  const wordsIn = (a, b) => allWords.filter((w) => w.start < b && w.end > a);

  return { lines, allWords, extras: json.extras || [], notes: json.notes || '', findLine, lineAt, displayLine, wordAt, findWord, wordsIn, norm };
}

function fakeWords(l) {
  const toks = String(l.text).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const span = (l.end - l.start) || 1;
  return toks.map((w, k) => ({ w, start: l.start + (k * span) / toks.length, end: l.start + ((k + 1) * span) / toks.length, conf: 0 }));
}
// The edit: scene windows anchored to lyric lines (by content) or sections, snapped to
// the beat grid. A window runs from its anchor to the next window's anchor.
//
// spec entry:
//   { plate: 'hook', params: { n: 1 },
//     at: { section: 'chorus1' }                      // section start
//       | { line: 'Seven-thirty', section?: 'verse1', nth?: 0, word?: 'bath' }  // line (or word) start
//       | { time: 0 },                                // absolute (only for the very first window)
//       | { t, why },                                 // a time a spec *function* derived from lyric content
//     snap: 'downbeat' | 'beat' | 'half' | 'none',    // default 'downbeat'
//     offsetBeats: 0,                                 // nudge after snapping
//     hero: { line, word, section, offsetBeats } | number (fraction of window)  // contact-sheet time
//   }

import { bisect } from './util.js';

export function buildTimeline(spec, { lyrics, beats }) {
  // A spec may be a function of the data, so clips can be derived from lyric content.
  if (typeof spec === 'function') spec = spec({ lyrics, beats });
  const resolveAnchor = (at, label) => {
    if (at == null) throw new Error(`timeline: plate ${label} has no anchor`);
    if (typeof at.time === 'number') return at.time;
    if (typeof at.t === 'number') return at.t;   // derived from lyric content by a spec function
    if (at.line != null || at.word != null) {
      const line = lyrics.findLine({ match: at.line ?? null, section: at.section ?? null, nth: at.nth ?? 0 });
      if (!line) throw new Error(`timeline: plate ${label}: no lyric line matches ${JSON.stringify(at)}`);
      if (at.word) {
        const w = lyrics.findWord(line, at.word, { nth: at.wordNth ?? 0, loose: at.loose ?? false });
        if (!w) throw new Error(`timeline: plate ${label}: no word "${at.word}" in line "${line.text}"`);
        return w.start;
      }
      return line.start;
    }
    if (at.section) {
      const s = beats.section(at.section);
      if (!s) throw new Error(`timeline: plate ${label}: no section "${at.section}" in audio.json`);
      return s.start;
    }
    throw new Error(`timeline: plate ${label}: cannot resolve anchor ${JSON.stringify(at)}`);
  };

  const windows = spec.map((e, idx) => {
    const label = `${e.plate}${e.params?.n ? `#${e.params.n}` : ''} (entry ${idx})`;
    const raw = resolveAnchor(e.at, label);
    let start = beats.snap(raw, e.snap ?? 'downbeat') + (e.offsetBeats || 0) * beats.beatDur;
    if (idx === 0) start = Math.min(start, 0);
    return { idx, id: `${e.plate}${e.params?.n ? e.params.n : ''}`, plate: e.plate, params: { ...(e.params || {}) }, anchor: raw, start, spec: e, label };
  });
  // Real (aligned) vocals don't land exactly on the grid, so two consecutive clauses can
  // snap to the same downbeat. Resolve collisions by tightening the snap for the later
  // window (beat, then half-beat), and as a last resort by placing it one beat after the
  // previous window. Windows stay monotonic and non-empty instead of throwing.
  for (let i = 1; i < windows.length; i++) {
    const prev = windows[i - 1], w = windows[i];
    if (w.start > prev.start) continue;
    const tries = [beats.snap(w.anchor, 'beat'), beats.snap(w.anchor, 'half'), w.anchor];
    let fixed = tries.find((t) => t > prev.start + 1e-6);
    if (fixed == null) fixed = prev.start + beats.beatDur;
    w.collided = { from: w.start, to: fixed };
    w.start = fixed;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < windows.length; i++) {
    windows[i].end = i + 1 < windows.length ? windows[i + 1].start : beats.duration;
    if (!(windows[i].end > windows[i].start)) {
      throw new Error(`timeline: window ${windows[i].label} is empty or out of order (${windows[i].start.toFixed(3)} -> ${windows[i].end.toFixed(3)})`);
    }
  }
  const collided = windows.filter((w) => w.collided);
  if (collided.length && typeof console !== 'undefined') {
    console.warn(`[timeline] ${collided.length} window(s) moved off a shared downbeat: ` +
      collided.slice(0, 6).map((w) => `${w.label} ${w.collided.from.toFixed(2)}->${w.collided.to.toFixed(2)}`).join('; ') + (collided.length > 6 ? ' ...' : ''));
  }
  // Lines belong to the window that contains a point just inside their start (pickups
  // before a snapped downbeat still belong to the plate they open).
  for (const w of windows) w.lines = [];
  for (const l of lyrics.lines) {
    const probe = l.start + Math.min(beats.beatDur, 0.3 * (l.end - l.start));
    const w = windowAt(probe);
    if (w) w.lines.push(l);
  }
  for (const w of windows) {
    w.dur = w.end - w.start;
    w.line = (k) => w.lines[k] || null;
    /** Find a word by text in this window's lines: word(match, {line: k | 'text', nth}) */
    w.word = (match, o = {}) => {
      const pool = o.line == null ? w.lines : typeof o.line === 'number' ? [w.lines[o.line]].filter(Boolean)
        : w.lines.filter((l) => l.n.includes(lyrics.norm(o.line)));
      let n = o.nth ?? 0;
      for (const l of pool) {
        for (const wd of l.words) {
          const nw = lyrics.norm(wd.w), m = lyrics.norm(match);
          if (nw === m || nw.includes(m)) { if (n-- === 0) return wd; }
        }
      }
      if (o.loose) for (const l of pool) for (const wd of l.words) if (/\d/.test(wd.w)) return wd;
      if (o.required === false) return null;
      throw new Error(`plate ${w.label}: no word "${match}" in its lines (${w.lines.map((l) => l.text).join(' / ')})`);
    };
    /** Time of a word's start (snapped to the beat grid if snap is given). */
    w.wordTime = (match, o = {}) => {
      const wd = w.word(match, o);
      if (!wd) return o.fallback ?? w.start;
      return o.snap ? beats.snap(wd.start, o.snap) : wd.start;
    };
    w.findLine = (match) => w.lines.find((l) => l.n.includes(lyrics.norm(match))) || null;
  }

  function windowAt(t) {
    const i = bisect(windows, t, (w) => w.start);
    return windows[Math.max(0, i)];
  }

  function heroTime(w) {
    const h = w.spec.hero;
    if (h == null) return w.start + w.dur * 0.5;
    if (typeof h === 'number') return w.start + w.dur * h;
    if (typeof h === 'function') return h(w);
    let t;
    if (h.word) t = w.wordTime(h.word, { line: h.line, nth: h.nth, loose: h.loose });
    else if (h.line) { const l = w.findLine(h.line); if (!l) throw new Error(`hero: no line ${h.line} in ${w.label}`); t = l.start; }
    else t = w.start;
    return t + (h.offsetBeats || 0) * beats.beatDur + (h.offset || 0);
  }

  return { windows, windowAt, heroTime };
}
// Page bootstrap shared by the preview and the headless render.
//   index.html?t=23            preview, scrubbed to 23 s
//   index.html?plate=hook&n=2  preview, jumped to that plate's hero time
//   index.html?render=1&w=540&h=960&fps=30&layout=9x16   headless mode: exposes window.__render
//   index.html?layout=16x9     preview the secondary layout

import { createEngine } from './engine.js';
import { startPreview } from '../preview.js';
import { fmtTime } from './util.js';

async function fetchJSON(url) {
  const r = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store' });
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`fetch ${url}: ${r.status}`);
  return r.json();
}

async function stylesheetsLoaded() {
  const links = [...document.querySelectorAll('link[rel="stylesheet"]')];
  await Promise.all(links.map((l) => (l.sheet ? null : new Promise((res) => {
    l.addEventListener('load', res, { once: true });
    l.addEventListener('error', res, { once: true });
  }))));
}

/**
 * Load every font the song declares and verify a real face (not a fallback) is loaded.
 * document.fonts.check() returns true for undeclared families, so look the faces up.
 */
export async function loadFonts(fonts, sample = 'AaBbGg 0123456789:.,!?\'"()') {
  await stylesheetsLoaded();
  const specs = fonts.map((f) => `${f.style || 'normal'} ${f.weight || 400} 64px "${f.family}"`);
  await Promise.all(specs.map((s) => document.fonts.load(s, sample).catch(() => null)));
  await document.fonts.ready;
  const faces = [...document.fonts];
  const missing = [];
  for (const f of fonts) {
    const want = Number(f.weight || 400), style = f.style || 'normal';
    const ok = faces.some((ff) => {
      if (ff.family.replace(/["']/g, '') !== f.family || ff.status !== 'loaded') return false;
      if ((ff.style || 'normal') !== style && !(style === 'normal' && ff.style === 'normal')) return false;
      const ws = String(ff.weight).split(/\s+/).map(Number);
      return ws.length === 2 ? want >= ws[0] && want <= ws[1] : ws[0] === want;
    });
    if (!ok) missing.push(`${f.family} ${f.weight || 400}${f.style && f.style !== 'normal' ? ' ' + f.style : ''}`);
  }
  return missing;
}

function toBase64(u8) {
  if (typeof u8.toBase64 === 'function') return u8.toBase64();
  let s = '';
  const CH = 0x8000;
  for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i += CH) s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, u8.subarray(i, i + CH));
  return btoa(s);
}

function fnv(u8) {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i += 7) h = Math.imul(h ^ u8[i], 16777619);
  return (h >>> 0).toString(16);
}


export async function boot(song, { base }) {
  const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const render = q.has('render');
  const status = document.getElementById('status');
  try {
    const missing = await loadFonts(song.fonts || []);
    if (missing.length) {
      const msg = `fonts missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`;
      if (render) throw new Error(msg);
      console.warn(msg);
      if (status) status.textContent = msg;
    }
    const dataBase = new URL(q.get('data') || 'data/', base);
    const [lyricsJson, audioJson] = await Promise.all([
      fetchJSON(new URL('lyrics.json', dataBase)),
      fetchJSON(new URL('audio.json', dataBase)),
    ]);
    if (render) return bootRender(song, { lyricsJson, audioJson, q });
    return startPreview(song, { lyricsJson, audioJson, q, base, missingFonts: missing });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    window.__render = { ready: false, error: String(e && e.stack || e) };
    if (status) status.textContent = 'ERROR: ' + (e && e.message || e);
    throw e;
  }
}

function bootRender(song, { lyricsJson, audioJson, q }) {
  const layout = q.get('layout') || song.defaultLayout || null;
  const d = (layout && song.layouts?.[layout]) || song.design || { w: 1920, h: 1080 };
  const w = Number(q.get('w') || d.w / 2), h = Number(q.get('h') || d.h / 2), fps = Number(q.get('fps') || 30);
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
  document.body.appendChild(canvas);
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true, alpha: false });
  const engine = createEngine({ song, lyricsJson, audioJson, width: w, height: h, fps, layout });
  const draw = (t) => engine.render(ctx, t);
  window.__render = {
    ready: true,
    w, h, fps, layout: engine.env.layout,
    duration: engine.duration,
    windows: engine.windows.map((x) => ({ id: x.id, plate: x.plate, params: x.params, start: x.start, end: x.end })),
    heroCells: () => engine.heroCells(),
    /** Raw RGBA of the frame at t, base64. */
    frame(t) { draw(t); return toBase64(new Uint8Array(ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data.buffer)); },
    /** Several frames at once (fewer CDP round trips). */
    frames(ts) { return ts.map((t) => this.frame(t)); },
    png(t) { draw(t); return canvas.toDataURL('image/png'); },
    hash(t) { draw(t); return fnv(new Uint8Array(ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data.buffer)); },
    time(t, n = 5) { const t0 = performance.now(); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) draw(t + i / fps); return (performance.now() - t0) / n; },
    /** Compose a contact sheet of cells [{t, label}] and return a PNG data URL. */
    sheet(cells, { cols = 4, cellW = 480, title = '' } = {}) {
      const cellH = Math.round((cellW * h) / w), labelH = 30, pad = 10, titleH = title ? 46 : 0;
      const rows = Math.ceil(cells.length / cols);
      const sc = document.createElement('canvas');
      sc.width = cols * (cellW + pad) + pad;
      sc.height = titleH + rows * (cellH + labelH + pad) + pad;
      const s = sc.getContext('2d');
      s.fillStyle = '#18181c';
      s.fillRect(0, 0, sc.width, sc.height);
      if (title) {
        s.fillStyle = '#e8e8ea';
        s.font = '600 22px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
        s.fillText(title, pad + 4, 31);
      }
      cells.forEach((c, i) => {
        const x = pad + (i % cols) * (cellW + pad), y = titleH + pad + Math.floor(i / cols) * (cellH + labelH + pad);
        draw(c.t);
        s.drawImage(canvas, x, y, cellW, cellH);
        s.fillStyle = '#e8e8ea';
        s.font = '600 16px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
        s.fillText(c.label, x + 2, y + cellH + 20);
        s.fillStyle = '#9a9aa2';
        s.textAlign = 'right';
        s.fillText(fmtTime(c.t), x + cellW - 2, y + cellH + 20);
        s.textAlign = 'left';
      });
      return sc.toDataURL('image/png');
    },
    env: engine.env,
  };
  return window.__render;
}

# lyric sheet parsing (Suno format)

STAGE_DIRECTIONS = re.compile(
    r"^(instrumental|.*\bsolo|.*\bbreak(down)?|beat drop|drop|fade( out)?|silence|pause|applause|"
    r"music|outro music|intro music|end)$", re.I)


def parse_sheet(text):
    """Suno lyric sheet -> list of lines.

    `[Section - notes]` header lines set the section name (everything after
    " - " is stripped).  Blank lines are ignored.  Every other line is a lyric
    line; its tokens (split on whitespace) keep their display spelling.  Tokens
    inside (...) are backing vocals (bv=True); parentheticals that are only a
    stage direction like "(instrumental)" are dropped from the line.

    Returns [dict(i, text, section, words=[dict(w, bv)])]."""
    lines, section = [], ""
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        s = raw.strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        if s.startswith("[") and s.endswith("]"):
            section = s[1:-1].split(" - ")[0].strip()
            continue
        # drop pure stage-direction parentheticals
        s = re.sub(r"\(([^()]*)\)", lambda m: "" if STAGE_DIRECTIONS.match(m.group(1).strip(" .")) else m.group(0), s)
        s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        words, depth = [], 0
        for tok in s.split(" "):
            opens, closes = tok.count("("), tok.count(")")
            bv = depth > 0 or opens > 0
            depth = max(0, depth + opens - closes)
            words.append(dict(w=tok, bv=bv))
        if not any(units(w["w"]) for w in words):
            continue  # punctuation-only line
        lines.append(dict(i=len(lines), text=s, section=section, words=words))
    return lines


def load_sheet(path):
    return parse_sheet(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# text normalisation: display token -> pronounceable sub-word units
# (letters a-z and internal apostrophes).  Used for the CTC targets AND for
# matching Whisper's words, so both sides speak the same spelling.

LETTER_NAMES = dict(a="ay", b="bee", c="see", d="dee", e="ee", f="eff", g="gee", h="aitch", i="eye", j="jay",
                    k="kay", l="el", m="em", n="en", o="oh", p="pee", q="cue", r="are", s="ess", t="tee", u="you",
                    v="vee", w="double you", x="ex", y="why", z="zee")
_ONES = "zero one two three four five six seven eight nine ten eleven twelve thirteen fourteen fifteen " \
        "sixteen seventeen eighteen nineteen".split()
_TENS = "_ _ twenty thirty forty fifty sixty seventy eighty ninety".split()
_ORD = dict(one="first", two="second", three="third", five="fifth", eight="eighth", nine="ninth", twelve="twelfth")


def num_words(n, year_style=True):
----


def write_json_timings(out, T):
    p = out / "align_debug.json"
    d = json.loads(p.read_text())
    d["timings_s"] = T.t
    common.write_json(p, d)


def write_outputs(out, lines, words, extras, notes, dbg, T, ww, mode):
    doc_lines = []
    for ln in lines:
        ws = [w for w in words if w["li"] == ln["i"]]
        assert " ".join(w["w"] for w in ws) == ln["text"], (ln["text"], [w["w"] for w in ws])
        items = []
        for w in ws:
            d = dict(w=w["w"], start=common.r3(w["start"]), end=common.r3(w["end"]), conf=round(float(w["conf"]), 2))
            if w.get("syl_t"):
                d["syl"] = [[common.r3(a), common.r3(b)] for a, b in w["syl_t"]]
            if w["bv"]:
                d["bv"] = True
            items.append(d)
        doc_lines.append(dict(i=ln["i"], text=ln["text"], start=items[0]["start"], end=items[-1]["end"], words=items))
    doc = dict(lines=doc_lines, extras=extras, notes=notes)
    common.write_json(out / "lyrics.json", doc)
    keep = ("li", "ti", "w", "bv", "start", "end", "conf", "conf_parts", "ctc_start", "ctc_end", "ctc_prob",
            "start_rule", "whisper", "src", "unit_texts")
    dbg_words = []
    for w in words:
        d = {k: w[k] for k in keep if k in w}
        if "units" in w:
            d["units"] = [dict(text=u["text"], ctc=[common.r3(u["start"]), common.r3(u["end"])],
                               start=common.r3(u.get("rstart", u["start"]))) for u in w["units"]]
        dbg_words.append(d)
    dbg.update(lines=[dict(i=ln["i"], section=ln["section"], text=ln["text"]) for ln in lines],
               words=dbg_words, whisper_words=ww, timings_s=T.t)
    common.write_json(out / "align_debug.json", dbg)
    nlow = sum(w["conf"] < 0.6 for w in words)
    print(f"[{mode}] wrote {out / 'lyrics.json'}: {len(doc_lines)} lines, {len(words)} words "
          f"({nlow} conf<0.6), {len(extras)} extras; timings {T.t}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# shared/align — lyric timings and beat grid for the renderer

Given a finished song (mp3/wav from Suno) and its Suno lyric sheet, produce

- `lyrics.json` — word-level lyric timings (+ syllables for multi-syllable words, backing-vocal flags, confidence)
- `audio.json` — bpm, beats, downbeats, sections, kicks, vocal onsets, loudness envelope

for the browser renderer (`shared/renderer/`).  Mirrors the `analysis/` directory of
[mexicat/pdoom-video](https://github.com/mexicat/pdoom-video), automated (no per-song hand-tuned anchors; manual
corrections go in a `--fix` JSON instead of the source).

## Install

Needs [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/) and `ffmpeg` on PATH (Apple Silicon Mac; Whisper runs on MLX).

```sh
uv sync --project shared/align                                   # Python 3.12 (pinned; torch has no 3.14 wheels yet)
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/prefetch_models.py   # optional: download all weights now
```

All commands below run from the repo root with absolute or repo-relative paths; nothing needs a `cd`.

## The three commands

```sh
# 1. full alignment (Demucs + karaoke vocal stems, CTC forced alignment, refinement, Whisper cross-check)
uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/align.py \
    --audio songs/2am-crying/audio/<file>.mp3 \
    --lyrics songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
    --out songs/2am-crying/data --plots
...
0-based as in lyrics.json; fixed words get conf 0.9 unless given), `--no-whisper`, `--force` (ignore caches),
`--gap-penalty`, `--margin` (Viterbi tuning, see `ctc.py`).

`align.py` also writes `<out>/align_debug.json`: sheet section per line, per-word CTC spans per unit, the
refinement rule that set each start, Whisper match, confidence parts, stem lag, per-stage timings.

`analyze.py` flags: `--sections song.json` (take bpm / beat grid / downbeats / sections from a known structure
file as the truth; only kicks, vocal onsets and loudness are computed; keys are read tolerantly:
`bpm|tempo`, `beats` (floats or `{t}`), `downbeats` or every `beats_per_bar`-th beat, `sections` with
`name|label` and `start|start_s` / `end|end_s`; the synth's `song.json` works as is), `--no-demucs` (kicks from
the mix, no vocal onsets), `--k N` (number of section clusters; default from the eigengap), `--plots`.
It also writes `<out>/audio_debug.json` (grid fit, downbeat phase scores, segmentation labels, timings).

## Running it on a real Suno download

1. Put the file at `songs/<song>/audio/<file>.mp3` (audio is gitignored; keep the original mp3: the browser
   plays the same file, so the timeline matches).
2. Run commands 1 and 3 with `--out songs/<song>/data` (and `--plots`).  Use the same `--lyrics` sheet you gave
   Suno; if Suno sang something different (it often repeats a chorus, drops an ad-lib or adds one), edit a copy
   of the sheet to what is actually sung first, since every sheet line is forced onto the audio.
3. Open `songs/<song>/data/qa/line_*.png`, sorted by the words with low `conf` (listed in the notes /
   `align_debug.json`).  Correct what is wrong in a `songs/<song>/data/fixes.json` and rerun command 1 with
   `--fix songs/<song>/data/fixes.json` (cached stems / emissions make the rerun take seconds).
4. Commit `lyrics.json` and `audio.json` (and `fixes.json`); the debug JSON and plots are optional.

## Outputs

`lyrics.json` (times in s, 3 decimals; conf 2 decimals):
`{lines: [{i, text, start, end, words: [{w, start, end, conf, syl?, bv?}]}], extras: [{start, end, desc}], notes}`.
`w` is the display token from the sheet (punctuation and parentheses kept; `" ".join(w) == text`).
`syl` = `[[start, end], ...]` per syllable, only for words with more than one syllable (orthographic syllables
timed by the CTC character path; tokens like `2am` / `5:40` / `BPM` split into their spoken parts).
`bv: true` only on backing-vocal words (parenthesised on the sheet).  `extras` = vocal activity (vocal stem) or
Whisper words not covered by any lyric word (ad-libs, unlisted backing vocals), with Whisper's text when it heard
something.  Sheet section headers are not in lyrics.json (no slot in the schema); they are per line in
`align_debug.json`.

`audio.json`: `{bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{name, start, end}], kicks, vocal_onsets,
loudness: {hop: 0.01, rms_db}, duration}`.  Detected sections are named `s1, s2, ...`.

**Timeline**: every time is on the gapless ffmpeg decode of the input file.  For an mp3 with a LAME/Xing tag,
ffmpeg trims the encoder delay + decoder delay (as Chrome / Firefox do), so the times match browser playback; the
test mp3 (libmp3lame, 576-sample delay) lands within ~1 ms of the wav.  Whether a Suno download carries the tag
is not verified yet: check the `mp3` field of `align_debug.json` / `audio_debug.json` on the first real file
(`{}` = no tag, in which case nothing is trimmed anywhere, which is also consistent).  Demucs is run through its Python API on that same decode, so the stems need no
shift (pdoom-video's -23 ms shift came from the demucs CLI decoding the mp3 itself); `stems.py` still measures the
stem-vs-mix lag by cross-correlation and logs it in the debug JSON (0 samples on the test songs).

## How it works

See the docstrings at the top of `align.py`, `ctc.py`, `stems.py`, `analyze.py`.  Short version:
----EVAL
# Alignment evaluation

Generated by `uv run --project shared/align python shared/align/tools/eval_alignment.py --run --md`.

**Test songs** (`tools/make_test_vocal.py`): every line of `songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt` (snapshot in
`test/vocal*/lyrics.txt`) is rendered separately with macOS `say` (lead voice Samantha, the parenthesised backing
vocals in Daniel, 185 wpm), silence-trimmed, and placed on the 128 BPM beat grid with known gaps, so the ground
truth of every line start / end is exact to the sample.  The vocal is mixed 3 dB below the backing RMS.
"BV-part start" = start of the second-voice part inside a line, i.e. the first word of `(I'm crying)`: the only
word-level ground truth available.  "Lead lines" excludes the four lines that are entirely backing-vocal
non-words (`(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)`, `(untz.)`).

### `test/vocal`: backing = synth:songs/2am-crying/synth/out/2am_crying.wav

48 lines, 180 s, vocal -3 dB vs. backing RMS.

| configuration | line-start mean | median | p95 | max | ≤100 ms | lead lines mean / p95 / max | line-end mean | BV-part start mean / p95 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| whisper-only | **248 ms** | 169 | 813 | 1513 | 17% | 190 / 451 / 571 | 297 | 639 / 801 |
| no-demucs | **91 ms** | 29 | 94 | 1919 | 96% | 27 / 67 / 99 | 169 | 66 / 82 |
| full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke) | **50 ms** | 16 | 34 | 1589 | 98% | 18 / 34 / 56 | 124 | 16 / 41 |
| full (Demucs + karaoke lead) | **19 ms** | 19 | 39 | 79 | 100% | 18 / 34 / 56 | 132 | 16 / 41 |
| full, mp3 input | **20 ms** | 21 | 34 | 79 | 100% | 18 / 34 / 34 | 87 | 25 / 48 |

Worst lines:
- whisper-only: L16 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` -1513 ms; L47 `(untz.)` -981 ms; L1 `(untz. untz. untz. untz.)` -942 ms
- no-demucs: L47 `(untz.)` +1919 ms; L16 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` -1162 ms; L11 `I can't reach it. You can reach it. Here's how I` +99 ms
- full, Demucs only (--no-karaoke): L47 `(untz.)` +1589 ms; L36 `The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:4` -56 ms; L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms
- full (Demucs + karaoke lead): L47 `(untz.)` +79 ms; L36 `The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:4` -56 ms; L17 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` +42 ms
- full, mp3 input: L47 `(untz.)` +79 ms; L17 `(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)` +42 ms; L12 `It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)` +34 ms

## Reading the numbers (written for the 2026-10-17 run)

- **The default full pipeline (Demucs + karaoke lead stem) meets the < 150 ms target on both songs**: line-start
  error ~19 ms (synth backing) / ~15 ms (loop) mean, p95 <= 40 ms, worst line 79 ms.  The remaining error is
  mostly the 20 ms CTC frame grid plus TTS words that start with a soft consonant.
- **Why the karaoke stem is on by default**: the synth demo contains voice-like parts (a formant "untz" one-shot
  and a formant baby-cry lead singing the hook), which Demucs keeps in its vocal stem.  With Demucs alone the last
  line `(untz.)` was aligned to the synth's "untz" 1.6 s too late; the karaoke model separates the TTS lead from
  those parts and the miss disappears.  On the loop backing (nothing voice-like) both variants are equal.  It costs
  ~80-100 s per song and 0.9 GB of weights.
- **mp3 vs. wav**: the same mix encoded with libmp3lame (LAME tag, 576-sample encoder delay) gives line starts
  within ~1 ms (mean) of the wav run, i.e. the gapless decode keeps the timeline intact.
- **no-demucs** is about 2x worse than full on clean material (31 vs. 15 ms) and fails on the non-word
  backing-vocal lines when the backing has voice-like parts (up to 1.9 s), since there is no separation and no
  signal refinement.
- **whisper-only** is a coarse preview (~250 ms mean, early bias): Whisper glues the silence before a line onto its
  first word (a duration cap on line-initial words removes part of that bias), and words it does not transcribe
  (non-words, repeated backing vocals) are interpolated.
- **analyze.py**: tempo and beat phase are exact on both songs (beat error 0.3-2 ms).  Downbeats are right on the
  synth song (chord changes on the one); on the loop every bar is identical (one chord, same drum pattern), so
  nothing marks bar 1 and the bar phase is a guess, here off by one beat (0% hit).  Section starts on the synth
  song land on the real boundaries at 7.5, 52.5, 82.5, 112.5, 142.5 and 157.5 s, add sub-section splits at
  15 / 30 / 90 s and place the two build-ups (true 37.5 and 97.5 s) 4 bars late at 45 / 105 s; on the loop (no
  structure) the boundaries just follow the vocal gaps.

## Caveats (read before trusting these numbers)

- Spoken TTS is much easier than a sung Suno vocal: clean consonant onsets, no melisma, no held vowels, no
  reverb tails, no doubled / harmonised lead, no ad-libs.  Expect real songs to be noticeably worse, mainly on
  long legato notes, buried choruses and ad-lib-heavy sections; check the `--plots` QA images and use `--fix`.
- The ground truth is line level only (plus the start of each backing-vocal part).  Word-level accuracy inside
  a line is not measured; `conf` (model agreement + CTC posterior + Whisper agreement) is the per-word signal.
- Both backings are synthetic; masking in a real mix (guitars, pads, reverb, sidechain) is different.
111:                n_beats=len(a["beats"]), sections=[s["start"] for s in a["sections"]], n_kicks=len(a["kicks"]),
137:            p = out / tag / "lyrics.json"
153:            print("   analyze.py:", {k: v for k, v in beats.items() if k != "sections"})

// Engine: wires audio.json + lyrics.json + the song's timeline into a pure render(t).
//
// A song module (see songs/<song>/video/song.js) provides:
//   design   { w, h }                 design coordinate space (1920x1080), or
//   layouts  { name: { w, h } }        several (e.g. '9x16', '16x9'); defaultLayout picks one
//   fonts    [{ family, weight, style? }]
//   timeline [spec entries]            see timeline.js
//   scenes   { plateId: { draw(ctx, S) } }
//   setup(env)                         optional; derive song-level curves (story clock...)
//   frame(ctx, t, env)                 composites one frame (scene, post, HUD, karaoke)
//   sheetCells(env)                    optional; extra contact-sheet cells [{ t, label }]
//
// Frames carry no state between calls: render(t) for any t, in any order, on any worker.

import { makeBeats } from './beats.js';
import { makeLyrics } from './lyrics.js';
import { buildTimeline } from './timeline.js';
import { clamp } from './util.js';

export function createEngine({ song, lyricsJson, audioJson, width, height, fps = 30, layout = null }) {
  const B = makeBeats(audioJson);
  const L = makeLyrics(lyricsJson, B);
  const T = buildTimeline(song.timeline, { lyrics: L, beats: B });
  layout = layout || song.defaultLayout || 'default';
  const design = song.layouts?.[layout] ?? song.design ?? { w: 1920, h: 1080 };
  if (song.layouts && !song.layouts[layout]) throw new Error(`unknown layout "${layout}" (have ${Object.keys(song.layouts).join(', ')})`);
  const W = design.w, H = design.h;
  for (const w of T.windows) if (!song.scenes[w.plate]) throw new Error(`timeline references unknown plate "${w.plate}"`);

  const env = { song, B, L, T, audio: audioJson, lyricsJson, W, H, fps, width, height, layout, duration: B.duration };

  /** Scene context for time t in window w. Song code adds palette (P) and frame state (F). */
  env.sceneContext = (t, w = T.windowAt(t), extra = {}) => {
    const lt = t - w.start;
    return {
      t, fps: env.fps, win: w, lt, dur: w.dur, p: clamp(lt / w.dur), params: w.params, n: w.params.n ?? 1,
      env, B, L, T, W, H,
      /** progress through [a, b] seconds relative to song time */
      beat: B.beatPos(t),
      ...extra,
    };
  };
  env.windowAt = (t) => T.windowAt(t);
  env.scene = (plate) => song.scenes[plate];

  song.setup?.(env);

  function render(ctx, t) {
    ctx.setTransform(width / W, 0, 0, height / H, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    ctx.filter = 'none';
    ctx.letterSpacing = '0px';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
    ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'miter';
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.save();
    song.frame(ctx, t, env);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function heroCells() {
    const cells = T.windows.map((w) => ({ t: T.heroTime(w), label: `${w.plate}${w.params.n ? ` n=${w.params.n}` : ''}`, plate: w.plate, id: w.id }));
    const extra = song.sheetCells?.(env) || [];
    return [...cells, ...extra].sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
  }

  return { env, render, duration: B.duration, windows: T.windows, heroCells };
}
