// clawd.js: Clawd, painted in watercolour and ink. The silhouette is blocky: a 10×6 body, four stubby legs, two little
// arm nubs and two slit eyes. `u` is the body unit (the body is 10u wide and 8u tall with legs). (x, y) is the point on
// the ground between the feet.
//
// Body-local coordinates in the front view, used by the o.draw / o.armL / o.armR hooks:
//   body x -5u..5u, y -8u..-2u; eye centres (±2.5u, -6u), each 1u wide and 2u tall; mouth near (0, -4.3u); legs reach y 0.
//   Arms pivot at (±4.9u, -4.5u) and are 2.2u long. o.armL / o.armR are called at the arm TIP in arm space (x runs
//   outward along the arm), so a held prop just draws around (0, 0).
//
// Everything here draws one frame. Motion comes from what you pass in: feel() / emotions() for moods, move() for
// dances, turn() for turns, and jump() / take() / stroll() / spring() from core.js for acting.

// ---------- views ----------
// Turns are drawn KEY VIEWS, like a cartoon model sheet, never a rotated 3D box. Every view faces screen-right;
// flip: true mirrors it to face left. Numbers are in u.
//   L, R: silhouette edges.  strip: the darker side face.  seam: the edge between faces.
//   face: where the features go. A front-view x maps to cx + x·fw; sides = which eyes show; mx = mouth x; bx = cheek x.
//   legs: [x, far] (far legs are darker).  arms: [pivotX, dir, which, layer] ('L' = o.aL / o.armL; layer 0 = behind the
//   body, 1 = in front of it, 2 = behind and in shadow).  hat / hw: hat offset and width.
const VIEWS = {
  front: { L: -5, R: 5, face: { cx: 0, fw: 1, sides: [-1, 1], mx: 0, bx: 3.6 }, hat: 0, hw: 1,
           legs: [[-4, 0], [-2, 0], [1, 0], [3, 0]], arms: [[-4.9, -1, 'L', 0], [4.9, 1, 'R', 0]] },
  q:     { L: -5, R: 5.1, strip: [-5, -2.3], seam: -2.3, face: { cx: 1.5, fw: .74, sides: [-1, 1], mx: .3, bx: 3.6 }, hat: .6, hw: 1,
           legs: [[-4.3, 1], [-1.8, 0], [.8, 0], [3.3, 0]], arms: [[5, 1, 'R', 2], [-4.7, -1, 'L', 1]] },
  side:  { L: -3.1, R: 3.1, face: { cx: 1.35, fw: .55, sides: [1], mx: 1.7, bx: 1 }, hat: .3, hw: .66,
           legs: [[-2.1, 1], [1.3, 1], [-2.6, 0], [.9, 0]], arms: [[1.6, 0, 'L', 1]] },
  qback: { L: -5.1, R: 5, strip: [2.3, 5], seam: 2.3, face: null, hat: -.4, hw: 1,
           legs: [[3.3, 1], [.8, 0], [-1.8, 0], [-4.3, 0]], arms: [[-5, -1, 'R', 2], [4.7, 1, 'L', 1]] },
  back:  { L: -5, R: 5, face: null, hat: 0, hw: 1, back: true,
           legs: [[-4, 0], [-2, 0], [1, 0], [3, 0]], arms: [[-4.9, -1, 'R', 0], [4.9, 1, 'L', 0]] },
};
// Key view for a heading in turns: 0 = front, .25 = facing right, .5 = back, .75 (or -.25) = facing left.
// Snaps to the nearest eighth, which is how a drawn turn works: front → q → side, one drawing per step.
function spinView(a) {
  const K = [['front', 0], ['q', 0], ['side', 0], ['qback', 0], ['back', 0], ['qback', 1], ['side', 1], ['q', 1]];
  const [view, f] = K[((Math.round(a * 8) % 8) + 8) % 8]; return { view, flip: !!f };
}
// A turn from heading a0 to a1 (in turns) between t0 and t1. It steps through the key views and smears the
// in-between drawings. Spread it into clawd(): clawd(x, y, u, { ...feel('happy', t), ...turn(t, 2, 2.2, 0, .25) }).
function turn(t, t0, t1, a0, a1) {
  const k = ease(seg(t, t0, t1)), v = spinView(lerp(a0, a1, k));
  return { ...v, smear: t > t0 && t < t1 ? .55 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI) : 0, smearDir: 0 };
}

// ---------- colour ----------
// Tints shift the body colour with the mood. tint: a name here or any hex colour; tintK: 0..1 strength.
const TINT = { pale: '#F6E6D2', flush: '#E23E36', blue: '#6D86BE', rosy: '#EF8EA8', green: '#98B25E', gold: '#F0BE46' };
function tintCols(o) {
  const c = { col: o.col || PAL.clay, dk: o.dk || PAL.clayDk, lt: o.lt || '#F5B394' };
  const tc = o.tint && (TINT[o.tint] || o.tint), k = clamp(o.tintK ?? 1);
  if (!tc || k <= 0) return c;
  return { col: mixCol(c.col, tc, .55 * k), dk: mixCol(c.dk, mixCol(tc, PAL.ink, .35), .5 * k), lt: mixCol(c.lt, mixCol(tc, '#FFFFFF', .4), .45 * k) };
}

// ---------- Clawd ----------
// Options (all optional):
//   pose:   dx, dy (in u; negative dy = up), sq (squash; negative stretches), rot (pivots at the feet), flip, sx, sy,
//           aL, aR (arm raise angles: 0 = straight out, + = up, - = down), walk (phase; the legs step), noLegs, noShadow
//   view:   front | q | side | qback | back (see VIEWS). smear 0..1 + smearDir ±1 (0 = both sides) for fast moves
//   face:   eyes (a name, or [left, right] for mismatched eyes), mouth, lookX / lookY (-1..1), squint 0..1, blush 0..1,
//           gloom 0..1 (dark forehead with gloom lines), lid 0..1 (lunchbox mouth, front view only), seed (blink timing)
//   colour: col / dk / lt, or tint + tintK
//   extras: hat, emote + emoteK (0..1 pop) + emoteAge (s since it appeared), draw(u, sw), armL(u, sw), armR(u, sw)
//   boil:   boilKey (a stable id for its boil seeds; defaults to call order, so set it if characters come and go mid-shot)
function clawd(x, y, u, o = {}) {
  // each part boils from its own seed (see boilSeed), so a moving arm never re-boils the body, or the next character
  const id = o.boilKey ?? ++CLAWD_N, rs = part => boilSeed(`clawd ${id} ${part}`);
  x += (o.dx || 0) * u;
  const V = VIEWS[o.view] || VIEWS.front;
  const dy = (o.dy || 0) * u, sq = (o.sq || 0) + (o.take || 0), sm = clamp(o.smear || 0);
  const sw = clamp(u / 15, .45, 2.4) * (o.swMul || 1), J = u * .07;
  const { col, dk, lt } = tintCols(o), far = mixCol(dk, PAL.ink, .22);

  rs('shadow');
  if (!o.noShadow) {
    const f = 1 - Math.min(.5, Math.abs(o.dy || 0) * .06), w = (V.R - V.L) / 10;
    paint(ellPts(x, y + u * .15, u * 5.6 * f * w, u * f, 22), { fill: PAL.ink, fillOp: 90, bleed: .25, tex: .3, border: .1, ink: null });
  }
  if (sm > .05) smearTrail(x, y + dy, u, V, sm, o.smearDir ?? (o.flip ? -1 : 1), col);

  push();
  translate(x, y + dy);
  if (o.rot) rotate(o.rot);
  scale((o.flip ? -1 : 1) * (o.sx ?? 1) * (1 + sq * .6) * (1 + sm * .35), (o.sy ?? 1) * (1 - sq));

  const arm = ([px, dir, which, layer]) => {
    rs('arm' + which);
    const a = which === 'L' ? (o.aL ?? .2) : (o.aR ?? .2), hook = which === 'L' ? o.armL : o.armR;
    // a raised (or dropped) arm slides its root out to the body's edge, so it stands beside the body instead of behind it
    push(); translate((px + dir * .55 * clamp((Math.abs(a) - .7) / .9)) * u, -4.5 * u);
    if (dir === 0) {   // side view: the near arm reaches forward past the front edge; a raises it, a < 0 lowers it
      translate(0, .3 * u); rotate(.7 - a);
      paint(rectPts(-.2 * u, -.45 * u, 2.3 * u, .9 * u, J * .6), { wash: col, washOp: 255, fill: dk, fillOp: 60, tex: .5, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .8 });
      if (hook) { translate(2.1 * u, 0); hook(u, sw); }
    } else {
      rotate(dir < 0 ? a : -a);
      paint(rectPts(dir < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0, -.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, J * .6), { wash: layer === 2 ? mixCol(col, dk, .4) : col, washOp: 255, fill: dk, fillOp: 60, tex: .5, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .8 });
      if (hook) { translate(dir * 2.2 * u, 0); if (dir < 0) scale(-1, 1); hook(u, sw); }
    }
    pop();
  };

  // arms behind, then legs (under the body), then the body, then arms in front
  V.arms.filter(a => a[3] !== 1).forEach(arm);
  if (!o.noLegs) V.legs.forEach(([lx, isFar], i) => {
    rs('leg' + i);
    let h = 2.2, sx = 0;
    if (o.walk != null) {
      if (o.view === 'side') {   // profile: diagonal pairs swing together, lifting as they swing forward
        const ph = (o.walk + [0, .5, .5, 0][i]) * TAU;
        sx = Math.sin(ph) * .55; h = 2.2 - Math.max(0, Math.cos(ph)) * .8;
      } else { const ph = Math.sin((o.walk + (i % 2 ? .5 : 0)) * TAU); if (ph > 0) h = 2.2 - ph * .9; }
    }
    paint(rectPts((lx + sx) * u, -2.4 * u, u, h * u, J * .6), { wash: isFar ? far : dk, washOp: 255, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .8 });
  });

  rs('body');
  const lid = V === VIEWS.front ? o.lid || 0 : 0;
  if (lid > .01) lunchbox(u, o, sw, J, lid, col, dk, lt);
  else {
    const L = V.L * u, R = V.R * u, wk = (V.R - V.L) / 10, body = rectPts(L, -8 * u, R - L, 6 * u, J);
    paint(body, { wash: col, washOp: 255, ink: null });
    const px = V.strip ? (V.strip[0] < 0 ? (V.seam + V.R) / 2 - 1.2 : (V.L + V.seam) / 2 - .6) : -1.6 * wk;
    paint(ellPts(px * u, -6.4 * u, 3.4 * u * (V.strip ? .75 : wk), 1.5 * u, 18, J * 2, -.08), { fill: lt, fillOp: V.back ? 80 : 120, bleed: .2, tex: .85, border: .8, ink: null });
    paint(rectPts(L + .2 * u, -3.8 * u, R - L - .4 * u, 1.6 * u, J), { fill: dk, fillOp: 120, bleed: .03, tex: .7, border: .5, ink: null });
    if (V.strip) {
      paint(rectPts(V.strip[0] * u, -8 * u, (V.strip[1] - V.strip[0]) * u, 6 * u, J * .5), { fill: dk, fillOp: 150, bleed: .04, tex: .6, border: .4, ink: null });
      inkLine([[V.seam * u + jit(J), -7.9 * u], [V.seam * u + jit(J), -5 * u], [V.seam * u + jit(J), -2.1 * u]], sw * .55, PAL.ink, 'inkfine', 0);
    }
    paint(body, { ink: PAL.ink, sw });
    if (o.gloom > .02) gloom(u, sw, V, o.gloom);
    if (V.face) {
      const F = V.face;
      push(); translate(F.cx * u, 0); scale(F.fw, 1);
      if (o.blush) blush(u, sw, F, o.blush === true ? 1 : o.blush);
      if (o.hat === 'mask' && F.sides.length > 1) paint([[-5.5 * u, -7.7 * u], [5.5 * u, -7.7 * u], [4.4 * u, -4.7 * u], [.6 * u, -5.4 * u], [-.6 * u, -5.4 * u], [-4.4 * u, -4.7 * u]], { wash: PAL.violet, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .7 });
      rs('eyes'); eyes(u, o, sw, F.sides, sm); rs('mouth');
      push(); translate(F.mx * u, 0); mouth(u, o.mouth, sw); pop();
      if (['cat', 'masq', 'bowtie'].includes(o.hat)) faceHat(u, o.hat, sw, F.sides);
      pop();

function eyes(u, o, sw, sides, smear = 0) {
  const kinds = Array.isArray(o.eyes) ? o.eyes : o.eyes === 'wink' ? ['happy', 'normal'] : [o.eyes || 'normal', o.eyes || 'normal'];
  const sqz = clamp(o.squint || 0);
  if (smear > .5) { for (const s of sides) inkLine([[s * 2.5 * u - .9 * u, -6 * u], [s * 2.5 * u + .9 * u, -6 * u]], sw * 1.4, PAL.ink, 'ink', 0); return; }
  if (sqz > .8) { for (const s of sides) inkLine([[s * 2.5 * u - .8 * u, -5.9 * u], [s * 2.5 * u + .8 * u, -5.9 * u]], sw, PAL.ink, 'ink', 0); return; }
  if (kinds[0] === 'shades') {   // sunglasses: two dark lenses, a bridge, arms running back along the head
    const P = pts => pts.map(([a, b]) => [a * u, b * u]);
    const lens = cx => P([[cx - 1.4, -7.1], [cx + 1.4, -7.1], [cx + 1.3, -6.2], [cx + .8, -5.35], [cx, -5.2], [cx - .8, -5.35], [cx - 1.3, -6.2]]);
    if (sides.length > 1) {
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) inkLine(P([[s * 3.9, -6.85], [s * 5, -6.95]]), sw * .8, PAL.ink, 'ink', 0);
      inkLine(P([[-1.15, -6.75], [0, -7.05], [1.15, -6.75]]), sw * .8, PAL.ink, 'ink', .5);
    } else inkLine(P([[1.1, -6.85], [-5, -6.95]]), sw * .8, PAL.ink, 'ink', 0);   // profile: the arm runs back to the ear
    for (const s of sides) {
      const cx = s * 2.5;
      paint(lens(cx), { wash: '#2A2740', ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .9, curv: .3 });
      inkLine(P([[cx - .85, -6.35], [cx - .25, -6.85]]), sw * .45, PAL.cream, 'inkfine', 0);   // glint
      inkLine(P([[cx - .45, -5.85], [cx - .05, -6.2]]), sw * .3, PAL.cream, 'inkfine', 0);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (sqz > 0) { push(); translate(0, -6 * u); scale(1 + sqz * .15, 1 - sqz); translate(0, 6 * u); }
  for (const s of sides) { push(); translate(s * 2.5 * u, -6 * u); eye(kinds[s < 0 ? 0 : 1], s, u, o, sw); pop(); }
  if (sqz > 0) pop();
}

// One eye, drawn around its centre. s = -1 for the left eye, 1 for the right.
function eye(e, s, u, o, sw) {
  const lx = (o.lookX || 0) * u * .5, ly = (o.lookY || 0) * u * .4;
  const slit = (w, h) => {
    paint(rectPts(-w / 2 * u + lx, -h / 2 * u + ly, w * u, h * u, u * .04), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null });
    if (u > 9) paint(ellPts(lx - w * .18 * u, ly - h * .29 * u, u * .17 * w, u * .24 * w, 10), { wash: PAL.cream, washOp: 230, ink: null });
  };
  const blinkOn = ['normal', 'look', 'wide'].includes(e) && ((T * .9 + (o.seed || 0) * 1.7) % 3.3) < .12;
  if (blinkOn) { inkLine([[-.7 * u, .5 * u], [.7 * u, .5 * u]], sw, PAL.ink, 'ink', 0); return; }
  const lineEye = (pts, w = 1.3, c = .2) => inkLine(pts.map(([a, b]) => [a * u, b * u]), sw * w, PAL.ink, 'ink', c);
  switch (e) {
    case 'normal': case 'look': slit(1, 2); break;
    case 'wide': slit(1.25, 2.7); break;
    case 'happy': lineEye([[-.9, .7], [0, -.5], [.9, .7]]); break;
    case 'closed': lineEye([[-.9, .2], [0, .6], [.9, .2]], 1.2, .4); break;
    case 'sleepy':
      paint(rectPts(-.5 * u + lx, .05 * u, u, .95 * u, u * .03), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null });
      lineEye([[-.8, .05], [0, -.1], [.8, .1]], 1.1, .4); break;
    case 'narrow': paint(rectPts(-.6 * u + lx, -.1 * u + ly, 1.2 * u, .7 * u, u * .03), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null }); break;
    case 'angry': case 'determined': {   // top edge slants DOWN toward the middle
      const hi = e === 'angry' ? .75 : .5, top = e === 'angry' ? -.7 : -.55;
      const P = [[-.65, s < 0 ? top : top + hi], [.65, s < 0 ? top + hi : top], [.65, 1], [-.65, 1]];
      paint(P.map(([a, b]) => [a * u + lx, b * u + ly]), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null });
      if (e === 'determined' && u > 9) paint(ellPts(lx - .2 * u, ly + .25 * u, u * .16, u * .2, 8), { wash: PAL.cream, ink: null });
      break;
    }
    case 'sad': case 'teary': {   // top edge slants UP toward the middle (worried)
      const P = [[-.6, s < 0 ? -.2 : -.85], [.6, s < 0 ? -.85 : -.2], [.6, .9], [-.6, .9]];
      paint(P.map(([a, b]) => [a * u + lx, b * u + ly]), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null });
      if (u > 9) paint(ellPts(lx - .18 * u, ly + .05 * u, u * .16, u * .22, 8), { wash: PAL.cream, ink: null });
      if (e === 'teary') {
        const w = Math.sin(T * 9 + s) * .06 * u;
        paint(ellPts(0, .95 * u + w, .95 * u, .38 * u, 14), { wash: PAL.sky, washOp: 210, fill: '#FFFFFF', fillOp: 60, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .4 });
        paint(ellPts(-.35 * u, .85 * u + w, .18 * u, .1 * u, 8), { wash: '#FFFFFF', ink: null });
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'cry': {   // squeezed shut, with tear streams running down the body and drops flicking off
      lineEye([[-.9, .45], [-.3, -.05], [.3, .15], [.9, -.2]].map(([a, b]) => [a * -s, b]), 1.3, .3);
      const R = []; for (let k = 0; k <= 5; k++) R.push([(s * .55 + Math.sin(T * 8 + k * 1.3 + s) * .12 * k / 5) * u, (.4 + k * .72) * u]);

      for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
        const ph = frac(T * 1.8 + k * .5 + (s > 0 ? .25 : 0)), p = arcPt([s * .9 * u, .2 * u], [s * 3.6 * u, 1.8 * u], 1.6 * u, ph);

      }
      break;
    }

    case 'shine':
      paint(ellPts(lx * .6, ly * .6, u * .78, u * 1.12, 16), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null });

      paint(rectPts(-.6 * u, -u, 1.2 * u, 2 * u, u * .05), { wash: '#FF2F4A', ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .5 });
      break;

function blush(u, sw, F, b) {
  for (const s of F.sides) {
    const bx = s * F.bx * u;
    paint(ellPts(bx, -4.6 * u, u * .85, u * .42, 14), { fill: PAL.rose, fillOp: 170 * clamp(b), bleed: .2, ink: null });
    if (b > .6) for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) inkLine([[bx - .5 * u + k * .4 * u, -4.35 * u], [bx - .3 * u + k * .4 * u, -4.85 * u]], sw * .4, mixCol(PAL.rose, PAL.ink, .3), 'inkfine', 0);
  }
}

// ---------- eyes ----------
// normal, look, wide, happy, closed, sleepy, wink, narrow, angry, determined, sad, teary, cry, squeeze, shine,
// scared, blank, spark, red, heart, x, swirl, dot, shades. o.eyes may be a pair: ['narrow', 'wide'].
function mouth(u, m, sw) {
  if (!m) return;
  const P = pts => pts.map(([a, b]) => [a * u, b * u]), dark = '#4A1F2A';
  const line = (pts, w = .8, c = .6) => inkLine(P(pts), sw * w, PAL.ink, 'ink', c);
  switch (m) {
    case 'o': paint(ellPts(0, -4.3 * u, u * .45, u * .5, 12), { wash: PAL.ink, ink: null }); break;
    case 'O': paint(ellPts(0, -4.1 * u, u * .8, u * .95, 14), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6 }); break;
    case 'smile': line([[-.8, -4.6], [0, -4.1], [.8, -4.6]]); break;
    case 'frown': line([[-.8, -4.1], [0, -4.6], [.8, -4.1]]); break;
    case 'grin': paint(P([[-1.3, -4.8], [1.3, -4.8], [.9, -3.9], [-.9, -3.9]]), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6, curv: .3 }); break;
    case 'flat': line([[-.7, -4.4], [.7, -4.4]], .8, 0); break;
    case 'wobble': line([[-1, -4.4], [-.5, -4.7], [0, -4.4], [.5, -4.7], [1, -4.4]], .7, .3); break;
    case 'cat': line([[-.9, -4.5], [-.45, -4.1], [0, -4.5], [.45, -4.1], [.9, -4.5]], .7, .5); break;
    case 'smirk': line([[-.8, -4.35], [.2, -4.3], [.9, -4.75]], .8, .5); break;
    case 'laugh':
      paint(P([[-1.4, -4.9], [1.4, -4.9], [1, -4], [0, -3.4], [-1, -4]]), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6, curv: .4 });
      paint(ellPts(0, -3.85 * u, .7 * u, .32 * u, 12), { wash: PAL.rose, ink: null }); break;
    case 'open':
      paint(ellPts(0, -4.25 * u, u * .75, u * .6, 14), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6 });
      paint(ellPts(0, -3.95 * u, u * .45, u * .2, 10), { wash: PAL.rose, ink: null }); break;
    case 'wail': {
      const w = Math.sin(T * 30) * .06;
      paint(P([[-1.5, -4.5 + w], [-.5, -4.9], [.5, -4.9 - w], [1.5, -4.5], [1.2, -3.3], [-1.2, -3.3]]), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6, curv: .3 });
      paint(ellPts(0, -3.6 * u, .8 * u, .25 * u, 12), { wash: PAL.rose, ink: null }); break;
    }
    case 'teeth':
      paint(rectPts(-1.3 * u, -4.85 * u, 2.6 * u, .95 * u, u * .04), { wash: PAL.cream, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6 });
      line([[-1.25, -4.38], [1.25, -4.38]], .4, 0);
      for (const tx of [-.65, 0, .65]) line([[tx, -4.8], [tx, -3.95]], .35, 0);
      break;
    case 'tongue':
      paint(P([[.05, -4.3], [.75, -4.3], [.72, -3.75], [.4, -3.55], [.08, -3.75]]), { wash: PAL.rose, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .5, curv: .5 });
      line([[-.8, -4.6], [0, -4.2], [.8, -4.5]]); break;
    case 'pout': line([[-.45, -4.2], [0, -4.5], [.45, -4.2]], 1, .6); line([[-.25, -4.0], [0, -3.9], [.25, -4.0]], .6, .6); break;
    case 'yawn':
      paint(ellPts(0, -4.1 * u, u * .6, u * 1.05, 14), { wash: dark, ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .6 });
      paint(ellPts(0, -3.45 * u, u * .38, u * .25, 10), { wash: PAL.rose, ink: null }); break;
  }
}

function hat(u, h, sw) {
  if (!h || h === 'mask' || h === 'masq' || h === 'bowtie') return;
  const P = pts => pts.map(([a, b]) => [a * u, b * u]);

  } else if (h === 'hard') {
    const d = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 12; i++) { const a = Math.PI + i / 12 * Math.PI; d.push([Math.cos(a) * 3.5 * u, -8 * u + Math.sin(a) * 3.3 * u]); }
    paint(d, { wash: '#F2C53D', ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .8 });
    paint(rectPts(-4.9 * u, -8.6 * u, 9.8 * u, .9 * u), { wash: '#F2C53D', ink: PAL.ink, sw: sw * .8 });
  } else if (h === 'crown') {

  } else if (h === 'halo') {
    brush.noFill(); brush.noWash(); brush.noHatch(); brush.set('ink', PAL.ochre, sw * 1.4);

function drawPhone(ctx, C) {
  const p = PHONE;
  // shadow
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sw * 0.3, p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sh * 0.75);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(p.sx - 400, p.sy - 200, p.sw + 800, p.sh + 400);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d10';
  rrect(ctx, p.sx - p.bezel, p.sy - p.bezel, p.sw + 2 * p.bezel, p.sh + 2 * p.bezel, p.r + p.bezel);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.save();
  rrect(ctx, p.sx, p.sy, p.sw, p.sh, p.r);
  ctx.clip();
  ctx.translate(p.sx, p.sy);
  ctx.scale(p.sw / CW, p.sh / CH);
  drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
  ctx.restore();
}

  },

def parse_sheet(text):
    """Suno lyric sheet -> list of lines.

    `[Section - notes]` header lines set the section name (everything after
    " - " is stripped).  Blank lines are ignored.  Every other line is a lyric
    line; its tokens (split on whitespace) keep their display spelling.  Tokens
    inside (...) are backing vocals (bv=True); parentheticals that are only a
    stage direction like "(instrumental)" are dropped from the line.

    Returns [dict(i, text, section, words=[dict(w, bv)])]."""
    lines, section = [], ""
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        s = raw.strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        if s.startswith("[") and s.endswith("]"):
            section = s[1:-1].split(" - ")[0].strip()
            continue
        # drop pure stage-direction parentheticals
        s = re.sub(r"\(([^()]*)\)", lambda m: "" if STAGE_DIRECTIONS.match(m.group(1).strip(" .")) else m.group(0), s)
        s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
        if not s:
            continue
        words, depth = [], 0
        for tok in s.split(" "):
            opens, closes = tok.count("("), tok.count(")")
            bv = depth > 0 or opens > 0
            depth = max(0, depth + opens - closes)
            words.append(dict(w=tok, bv=bv))
        if not any(units(w["w"]) for w in words):
            continue  # punctuation-only line
        lines.append(dict(i=len(lines), text=s, section=section, words=words))
    return lines

def section_id(name):
    """Header name -> base section id: the text before " - ", lowercased, spaces
    dropped ("Verse 1" -> "verse1", "Pre-Chorus" -> "pre-chorus"); "Final Chorus" -> "final"."""
    base = re.sub(r"\\s+", "", name.split(" - ")[0].strip().lower())
    return "final" if base == "finalchorus" else base


def section_ids(headers):
    """Section id per header occurrence, in sheet order.  A plain name (no trailing
    digit) that occurs more than once is numbered by occurrence: Chorus, Chorus ->
    chorus1, chorus2.  Names that occur once, or already carry a number, are kept."""
    base = [section_id(h) for h in headers]
    count, seen, out = {}, {}, []
    for b in base:
        count[b] = count.get(b, 0) + 1
    for b in base:
        if count[b] > 1 and not re.search(r"\\d$", b):
            seen[b] = seen.get(b, 0) + 1
            out.append(f"{b}{seen[b]}")
        else:
            out.append(b)
    return out
'''
assert old2 in s; s = s.replace(old2, new2)
p.write_text(s)

// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.

  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();
}

Write the sheet parser for the renderer core.

Writeshared/renderer/core/sheet.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/core/sheet.js
// Suno lyric sheet -> section ids per line, for timing data that has no `section` field.
// Same rules as shared/align/common.py (parse_sheet / section_ids):
//   `[Section - notes]` headers name the section: the text before " - ", lowercased,
//   spaces dropped ("Verse 1" -> verse1); "Final Chorus" -> final; a plain name (no
//   trailing digit) that occurs more than once is numbered by occurrence (Chorus, Chorus
//   -> chorus1, chorus2). Blank lines are skipped, pure stage-direction parentheticals
//   ("(instrumental)") are dropped from a line, punctuation-only lines are skipped.

const STAGE = /^(instrumental|.*\bsolo|.*\bbreak(down)?|beat drop|drop|fade( out)?|silence|pause|applause|music|outro music|intro music|end)$/i;

export function sectionId(name) {
  const base = String(name).split(' - ')[0].trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
  return base === 'finalchorus' ? 'final' : base;
}

/** Section id per header occurrence, in sheet order. */
export function sectionIds(headers) {
  const base = headers.map(sectionId);
  const count = {}, seen = {};
  for (const b of base) count[b] = (count[b] || 0) + 1;
  return base.map((b) => {
    if (count[b] > 1 && !/\d$/.test(b)) { seen[b] = (seen[b] || 0) + 1; return `${b}${seen[b]}`; }
    return b;
  });
}

/** Sheet text -> [{ text, section, header }] lyric lines in sheet order. */
export function parseSheet(text) {
  const raw = [], headers = [];
  for (const r of String(text).split(/\r?\n/)) {
    let s = r.trim();
    if (!s) continue;
    if (s.startsWith('[') && s.endsWith(']')) { headers.push(s.slice(1, -1).split(' - ')[0].trim()); continue; }
    s = s.replace(/\(([^()]*)\)/g, (m, inner) => (STAGE.test(inner.trim().replace(/^[ .]+|[ .]+$/g, '')) ? '' : m)).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!s || !/[a-z0-9]/i.test(s)) continue;
    raw.push({ text: s, h: headers.length - 1 });
  }
  const ids = sectionIds(headers);
  return raw.map((l) => ({ text: l.text, section: l.h >= 0 ? ids[l.h] : '', header: l.h >= 0 ? headers[l.h] : '' }));
}
result

from pathlib import Path

s = p.read_text()
old = '''import { bisect } from './util.js';
'''
new = '''import { bisect } from './util.js';
import { parseSheet } from './sheet.js';
'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old = '''export function makeLyrics(json, beats) {
  const lines = (json.lines || []).map((l, k) => {
    const words = (l.words && l.words.length ? l.words : fakeWords(l)).map((w, j) => ({ ...w, j }));
    return { ...l, i: l.i ?? k, words, n: norm(l.text) };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  lines.forEach((l, k) => { l.k = k; });
  // Section membership from audio.json (robust to alignment output without a section field).
  if (beats) {
    for (const l of lines) {
      const probe = l.start + Math.min(beats.beatDur, 0.3 * (l.end - l.start));
      l.section = beats.sectionAt(probe)?.name ?? l.section;
    }
  }
'''
new = '''/**
 * Section ids for lines that have none, from the lyric sheet: lines are matched to sheet
 * lines by normalized text, in sheet order (so repeated choruses land on their own
 * occurrence); a line the sheet does not have inherits the previous line's section.
 * Returns the number of lines that got a section.
 */
export function sectionsFromSheet(lines, sheetText) {
  const sheet = parseSheet(sheetText).map((x) => ({ ...x, n: norm(x.text) }));
  const order = [...lines].sort((a, b) => (a.i ?? 0) - (b.i ?? 0) || a.start - b.start);
  let cur = 0, prev = null, n = 0;
  for (const l of order) {
    let j = cur;
    while (j < sheet.length && sheet[j].n !== l.n) j++;
    const sec = j < sheet.length ? sheet[j].section : prev;
    if (j < sheet.length) cur = j + 1;
    if (!l.section && sec) { l.section = sec; n++; }
    prev = l.section || sec;
  }
  return n;
}

/**
 * json: lyrics.json. beats: makeBeats(audio.json). sheetText: the lyric sheet, used only
 * when lines carry no `section`. Section precedence: the line's own `section` (the aligner
 * writes the sheet header's id), then the sheet, then audio.json's sections (a last
 * resort: detected sections are named s1, s2, ... and match no clip rule).
 */
export function makeLyrics(json, beats, { sheetText = null, sheetName = '' } = {}) {
  const lines = (json.lines || []).map((l, k) => {
    const words = (l.words && l.words.length ? l.words : fakeWords(l)).map((w, j) => ({ ...w, j }));
    return { ...l, i: l.i ?? k, words, n: norm(l.text) };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  lines.forEach((l, k) => { l.k = k; });
  const warn = (m) => { if (typeof console !== 'undefined') console.warn(m); };
  const missing = () => lines.filter((l) => !l.section).length;
  let sectionSource = 'lyrics.json';
  if (missing() && sheetText) {
    const n0 = missing();
    sectionsFromSheet(lines, sheetText);
    sectionSource = 'sheet';
    warn(`[lyrics] ${n0} of ${lines.length} lines have no "section" in lyrics.json: derived from the lyric sheet ${sheetName || ''}`.trim() +
      ` (${[...new Set(lines.map((l) => l.section))].join(', ')})${missing() ? `; ${missing()} still unassigned` : ''}`);
  }
  if (missing() && beats) {
    // last resort: section membership from audio.json
    const n0 = missing();
    for (const l of lines) {
      if (l.section) continue;
      const probe = l.start + Math.min(beats.beatDur, 0.3 * (l.end - l.start));
      l.section = beats.sectionAt(probe)?.name ?? l.section;
    }
    sectionSource = 'audio.json';
    const names = [...new Set(beats.sections.map((s) => s.name))];
    warn(`[lyrics] ${n0} line(s) have no "section" and no lyric sheet was loaded: using audio.json sections (${names.join(', ')})` +
      (names.every((x) => /^s\\d+$/.test(x)) ? '. These are detected sections (s1, s2, ...): no clip rule keyed on a section name will match' : ''));
  }
'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old = '''  return { lines, allWords, extras: json.extras || [], notes: json.notes || '', findLine, lineAt, displayLine, wordAt, findWord, wordsIn, norm };'''
new = '''  return { lines, allWords, extras: json.extras || [], notes: json.notes || '', sectionSource, findLine, lineAt, displayLine, wordAt, findWord, wordsIn, norm };'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
p.write_text(s)

p = Path(R + "core/boot.js"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''export async function boot(song, { base }) {'''
new = '''/**
 * The lyric sheet named in audio.json (`lyrics_sheet`), fetched only when some lyrics.json
 * line has no `section` (older alignment output). The name may be relative to the data
 * dir, to the song root (the video dir's parent: "suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt") or to the repo
 * root ("songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt"); the first that loads wins.
 */
export async function loadSheet(lyricsJson, audioJson, { dataBase, base }) {
  const lines = lyricsJson.lines || [];
  if (lines.every((l) => l.section)) return null;
  const name = audioJson.lyrics_sheet || lyricsJson.lyrics_sheet;
  if (!name) {
    console.warn('[lyrics] lyrics.json lines have no "section" and audio.json names no lyrics_sheet to derive them from');
    return null;
  }
  const clean = String(name).replace(/^\\/+/, '');
  const tries = (clean.includes('/') ? [new URL('/' + clean, location.origin), new URL('../' + clean, base)] : [new URL('../' + clean, base), new URL('/' + clean, location.origin)])
    .concat([new URL(clean, dataBase)]);
  for (const u of tries) {
    try {
      const r = await fetch(u, { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (r.ok) return { name: clean, text: await r.text(), url: String(u) };
    } catch { /* try the next */ }
  }
  console.warn(`[lyrics] lyrics_sheet "${name}" not found (tried ${tries.map(String).join(', ')})`);
  return null;
}

export async function boot(song, { base }) {'''

BashAnchor the outro loop on the Hi line and generalize the loop check6:26 AM
input
description Anchor the outro loop on the Hi line and generalize the loop check
python3 - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
V = "/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/"
p = Path(V + "story.js"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''  // The outro is the intro again, frame-exactly: quantize to the frame grid so outro frame
  // k shows intro frame j (same t -> same pixels, every input included).
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!outro || t < outro.start) return t;
    const fps = env.fps || 30;
    return intro.start + Math.round((t - outro.start) * fps) / fps;
  }'''
new = '''  // The outro is the intro again, frame-exactly: outro frame k shows intro frame k - shift
  // (same t -> same pixels, every input included). The shift lines the outro's "Hi" up with
  // the intro's: a real vocal does not start them at the same offset into their windows
  // (Suno runE2: 2.80 s into the intro, 0.18 s into the outro), and the caption is the
  // lyric. It is a whole number of frames, so every outro frame is an intro frame.
  const fps = env.fps || 30;
  const hiIntro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'intro' });
  const hiOutro = L.findLine({ match: 'Hi', section: 'outro' });
  const shiftFrames = outro
    ? Math.round(((hiIntro && hiOutro) ? hiOutro.start - hiIntro.start : outro.start - intro.start) * fps)
    : 0;
  function loopTime(t) {
    if (!outro || t < outro.start) return t;
    return Math.max(intro.start, (Math.round(t * fps) - shiftFrames) / fps);
  }'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old = '''  return { clauses, loopTime, windowFor, clauseAt, clock, battery, rec, recStart, intro, outro };'''
new = '''  return { clauses, loopTime, loopShift: shiftFrames / fps, windowFor, clauseAt, clock, battery, rec, recStart, intro, outro };'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new); p.write_text(s)

p = Path(V + "song.js"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''  /** Extra contact-sheet cells: stages of the long take and the grid filling in. */'''
new = '''  /** The song time whose frame an outro frame repeats (render.mjs --check compares the two). */
  loopSource(env, t) { return env.story.loopTime(t); },
  /** Extra contact-sheet cells: stages of the long take and the grid filling in. */'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new); p.write_text(s)

p = Path("/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/core/boot.js"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''    heroCells: () => engine.heroCells(),'''
new = '''    heroCells: () => engine.heroCells(),
    /** For a looping song: the time whose frame t repeats (null if the song does not say). */
    loopSource: (t) => (song.loopSource ? song.loopSource(engine.env, t) : null),'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new); p.write_text(s)

p = Path("/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''    for (const j of [3, 17, 40, 60, 90]) {
      const k = outroStartFrame + j;
      if (k / fps >= info.duration) continue;
      const jj = Math.round(k - outro.start * fps); // the intro frame the loop maps onto
      const a = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), jj / fps);
      const b = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), k / fps);
      if (a !== b) { fails++; log(`LOOP MISMATCH: outro frame ${k} vs intro frame ${jj}`); }
    }'''
new = '''    let shown = null;
    for (const j of [3, 17, 40, 60, 90, 150]) {
      const k = outroStartFrame + j;
      if (k / fps >= info.duration) continue;
      // the intro frame the loop maps onto: the song says (e.g. anchored on a lyric), else outro start -> intro start
      const src = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.loopSource(x), k / fps);
      const jj = src == null ? Math.round(k - outro.start * fps) : Math.round(src * fps);
      if (src != null && (Math.abs(src * fps - jj) > 1e-6 || src >= outro.start)) { fails++; log(`LOOP FAIL: outro frame ${k} maps to t=${src}, not an intro frame`); continue; }
      const a = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), jj / fps);
      const b = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), k / fps);
      if (a !== b) { fails++; log(`LOOP MISMATCH: outro frame ${k} vs intro frame ${jj}`); }
      shown ??= { k, jj };
    }
    if (shown) log(`loop mapping: outro frame ${shown.k} (${(shown.k / fps).toFixed(3)}s) -> intro frame ${shown.jj} (${(shown.jj / fps).toFixed(3)}s), shift ${((shown.k - shown.jj) / fps).toFixed(3)}s`);'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new); p.write_text(s)

lowercased, spaces dropped (`Verse 1` -> `verse1`), `Final Chorus` -> `final`, and a plain name that occurs more
than once numbered by occurrence (`Chorus`, `Chorus` -> `chorus1`, `chorus2`); lines before any header get `""`.

from pathlib import Path

195.56 s, 133.7 BPM), aligned by `shared/align` (`align.py` + `analyze.py`, output in
`../audio/runE2/data/`, copied here). The mock timing it replaced (synth demo / 128 BPM plan) is in
`data-mock/` (`?data=data-mock/` in the preview, `--data data-mock` in render.mjs).

- `data/lyrics.json`: `{ lines: [{ i, text, start, end, section, words: [{ w, start, end, conf, syl?, bv? }] }], extras, notes }`
  (mexicat/pdoom-video schema). **`section` is load-bearing**: the sheet header's id, `intro, chorus1, verse1,
  chorus2, verse2, bridge, final, outro` (the aligner writes it; naming in `shared/align/README.md`). Clip
  selection keys on it: verse clauses cut on the half bar, every `bridge` clause goes into the one long take,
  every `final` clause into the grid, and the `outro` "Hi" line is the loop.
- `data/audio.json`: `{ bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{ name, start, end }], kicks, duration }` plus
  optional fields: `beats_per_bar`, `hats` (offbeat onsets), `quiet` (silent spans), `risers`, `vocal_onsets`,
  `loudness`, `source` (`"synth (...)"`, `"plan"`, or a take with its file in parentheses: render.mjs and the
  preview pick the audio from it), `lyrics_sheet` (the sheet, relative to the song root `songs/2am-crying/`
  or to the repo root). analyze.py's `sections` are detected structure named `s1..s11`; nothing selects
  clips by them.

Section precedence in the engine (`shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js`): a line's own `section`, else the
section derived from the sheet named in `audio.json.lyrics_sheet` (same naming, matched by line text in
order; `[lyrics] ... derived from the lyric sheet` warning), else audio.json's sections (warning; with
`s1..s11` no section plate can match). render.mjs prints at start-up where the sections came from and
which section plates exist, and says `MISSING` for an absent one:

```
[render] line sections from lyrics.json: intro, chorus1, verse1, chorus2, verse2, bridge, final, outro
[render] section plate ok: bridge long take -> nap:long 2:06.13-2:24.09 (18.0s)
[render] section plate ok: final chorus grid -> grid 2:24.09-3:08.98 (44.9s)
[render] section plate ok: outro loop -> hello 3:08.98-3:15.56 (6.6s)
```

`--check` fails (exit 2) when the outro loop is missing or an outro frame differs from its intro frame.
The loop is anchored on the "Hi" line: outro frame k shows intro frame k - shift, the shift being the
whole-frame distance between the two "Hi"s (186.367 s on runE2; the outro window starts 0.18 s before its
"Hi", the intro 2.80 s before), so the caption stays on the vocal.

`tools/mock_timing.mjs` builds the mock pair (`--plan` ignores the synth); write it to `data-mock/`. Clips
are derived from lyric content, so a new take only needs its `lyrics.json`/`audio.json` in `data/`. Watch the
render log for `[clips] ... matched no rule` (a clause whose text drifted falls back to the closeup clip) and
`[timeline] ... moved off a shared downbeat` (two clauses snapped to the same downbeat; the later one moves to
the next beat).

`render.mjs` options: `--from/--to` seconds, `--workers N` (default ~60% of cores),
`--crf 16`, `--preset medium`, `--audio file`, `--force` (allow audio/timing length mismatch),
`--check` (determinism: ordered vs shuffled frames across workers; loop: outro frames equal
intro frames). Video is libx264 CRF 16, yuv420p, tagged BT.709; audio AAC 192k.

description Commit the section fix
R=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop; rm -rf $R/songs/2am-crying/video/renders/nosheet; git -C $R status --short; git -C $R add shared/align/align.py shared/align/common.py shared/align/README.md shared/renderer/core/sheet.js shared/renderer/core/lyrics.js shared/renderer/core/engine.js shared/renderer/core/boot.js shared/renderer/preview.js shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs songs/2am-crying/audio/runE2/data/lyrics.json songs/2am-crying/video/data/lyrics.json songs/2am-crying/video/clips.js songs/2am-crying/video/story.js songs/2am-crying/video/song.js songs/2am-crying/video/README.md && git -C $R commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
align + renderer: lyrics.json lines carry their sheet section; bridge long take, final grid and outro loop return on the Suno take

The aligned runE2 lyrics.json had no `section`, and the engine overwrote every line's
section with audio.json's detected s1..s11 anyway, so no bridge long take (no bottle
during the milk verse), no final-chorus grid, no outro loop, and verse clauses cut on
the downbeat instead of the half bar.

- shared/align: parse_sheet gives each line `sid` (Verse 1 -> verse1, Chorus x2 ->
  chorus1/chorus2, Final Chorus -> final); align.py writes it as `section`. runE2
  re-aligned from the cache: timings identical, sections added; copied to video/data.
- renderer: a line's own section wins; else derived from audio.json.lyrics_sheet
  (core/sheet.js, same naming, warning); audio.json sections only as a last resort.
- render.mjs prints where sections came from and which section plates exist (MISSING
  otherwise), captures console.warn (Puppeteer type 'warn', which was being dropped),
  takes --data, and --check fails when the declared outro loop is missing.
- the outro loop is anchored on the "Hi" line (whole-frame shift), so its caption
  stays on the vocal; --check asks the page which intro frame each outro frame repeats.
- the preview plays the take named in audio.json source.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF

export function rrect(ctx, x, y, w, h, r) {
  r = Math.max(0, Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2));
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
  ctx.closePath();
}

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/baby.js
// Baby Clawd: the Claude Code mascot as a baby. Clawd is a blocky clay body (10 units wide,
// 6 tall) on four stubby legs with two arm nubs and tall rounded-rectangle eyes; the baby is
// chubbier and nearly square, with rounder corners, shorter legs, bigger and lower eyes with
// a big highlight, a single curl on top and blush. Night: a sleep sack that wraps the legs
// (a soft bag with a zipper). Day: a onesie band across the lower body with two snaps.
// 'naked' is the bare clay body (after the bath), 'towel' adds a cape. A pacifier for the
// quiet and sleeping moments. One arm nub always stretches toward a bottom corner of the
// frame, holding the phone (we see the nub, never the phone).
//
// drawBaby(ctx, o): o.x, o.y face centre, o.r scale (design px; the body is 2.5r wide), o.C
// colours (skin = clay, skinShade, skinLight, ink, line, mouth, white, cloth, clothShade,
// towel, stain, blush, tear, paci), plus the pose fields in BABY_DEFAULTS. No state, no
// randomness: the same o always draws the same pixels.

import { clamp, lerp, TAU } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix, rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

export const BABY_DEFAULTS = {
  r: 300, tilt: 0,
  outfit: 'onesie',          // 'onesie' | 'sack' | 'naked' | 'towel'
  eyes: 'open',              // 'open' | 'half' | 'closed' | 'squeeze' | 'wide' | 'focus'
  open: 1,                   // continuous eyelid 0..1 (overrides when < 1 for 'open'/'wide')
  look: [0, 0],              // eye / highlight offset (-1..1)
  mouth: 'line',             // 'line' | 'o' | 'smile' | 'grin' | 'none' | 'press'
  mouthOpen: 1,
  brows: null,               // null | 'furrow' | 'up'  ('press' implies 'furrow')
  phoneArm: 'left',          // which bottom corner the phone nub reaches for
  free: { mode: 'down' },    // free nub: 'down' | 'wave' | 'up' | 'point' | 'hold' | 'none'
  stain: false, tears: 0, blush: 0,
  pacifier: 0,               // 0..1: the pacifier over the mouth (quiet and sleeping moments)
  flash: 0,                  // 0..1 hot-spot of the phone flash on the face
};

/** Geometry in units of r around the face centre (scenes place props and glows with it). */
export const BABY_GEOM = {
  body: { l: -1.25, r: 1.25, t: -1.0, b: 1.6, rad: 0.56, bulge: 0.05 },
  eye: { dx: 0.44, dy: 0.0, w: 0.3, h: 0.44, rad: 0.12 },
  mouthY: 0.47,
  cheek: { dx: 0.8, dy: 0.36 },
  arm: { x: 1.16, y: 0.46, t: 0.3 },
  legs: [-0.86, -0.38, 0.38, 0.86], legW: 0.3, legH: 0.3,
  sack: { t: 0.74, b: 2.08, w: 1.33 },
  band: 0.95,
};
const G0 = BABY_GEOM;

const ROSE = '#ee8a8a', SKY = '#8fd0f6';

/** The body outline: a rounded square whose sides bow out a little (chubby). */
function bodyPath(ctx, r) {
  const { l, r: rt, t, b, rad, bulge } = G0.body;
  const L = l * r, R = rt * r, T = t * r, B = b * r, k = rad * r, q = bulge * r;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(L + k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, T - q, R - k, T);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, T, R, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R + q, (T + B) / 2, R, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(R, B, R - k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, B + q, L + k, B);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, B, L, B - k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L - q, (T + B) / 2, L, T + k);
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(L, T, L + k, T);
  ctx.closePath();
}

function rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, k) {
  k = Math.max(0, Math.min(k, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2));
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + k, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, k);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, k);
  ctx.closePath();
}

/**
 * A nub from `a` to `b` (thickness t0 at the root, t1 at the tip). The root end sits inside
 * the body and is not outlined, so the nub grows out of the body silhouette.
 */
function nub(ctx, a, b, t0, t1, fill, line, lw, { tipRound = 0.35, rootOpen = true } = {}) {
  const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  const nx = -dy / d, ny = dx / d;
  const h0 = t0 / 2, h1 = t1 / 2, c = Math.min(h1 * 2 * tipRound, d * 0.4);
  const ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d;
  const p = (s, h, along = 0) => [a[0] + ux * s * d + nx * h + ux * along, a[1] + uy * s * d + ny * h + uy * along];
  const A = p(0, h0), Bq = p(1, h1, -c), Cq = p(1, -h1, -c), D = p(0, -h0);
  const T1 = p(1, h1), T2 = p(1, -h1);
  const outline = () => {
    ctx.moveTo(A[0], A[1]);
    ctx.lineTo(Bq[0], Bq[1]);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T1[0], T1[1], (T1[0] + T2[0]) / 2, (T1[1] + T2[1]) / 2);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(T2[0], T2[1], Cq[0], Cq[1]);
    ctx.lineTo(D[0], D[1]);
  };
  ctx.beginPath(); outline(); ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
  if (line) {
    ctx.beginPath(); outline();
    if (!rootOpen) ctx.closePath();
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  let mode = o.eyes;
  const open = clamp(o.open);
  if ((mode === 'open' || mode === 'wide' || mode === 'half') && open < 0.18) mode = 'closed';
  const lx = o.look[0] * 0.07 * r, ly = o.look[1] * 0.06 * r;
  const ex = k * E.dx * r, ey = E.dy * r;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.fillStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  if (mode === 'closed') {
    // sleeping: a soft downward arc
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.055 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.15 * r, ex + 0.16 * r, ey + 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
    return;
  }
  if (mode === 'squeeze') {
    // squeezed shut: > <
    ctx.lineWidth = 0.062 * r;
    const w = 0.13 * r, h = 0.135 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex + k * w, ey - h); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * w * 0.9, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + k * w, ey + h); ctx.stroke();
    return;
  }
  if (mode === 'focus') {
    // concentration: a narrow flat slit
    rr(ctx, ex - 0.16 * r + lx * 0.5, ey + 0.02 * r, 0.32 * r, 0.12 * r, 0.05 * r); ctx.fill();
    return;
  }
  const s = mode === 'wide' ? 1.14 : 1;
  const w = E.w * s * r, h = E.h * s * r;
  const x0 = ex - w / 2 + lx, y0 = ey - h / 2 + ly;
  const lid = mode === 'half' ? Math.min(open, 0.5) : open;
  const cut = y0 + h * (1 - lid);
  ctx.save();
  if (lid < 1) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(x0 - w, cut, w * 3, h * 2); ctx.clip(); }
  rr(ctx, x0, y0, w, h, E.rad * s * r); ctx.fill();
  // the highlight: a big dot up and to the left, a small one below it
  const hx = x0 + w * 0.33 + o.look[0] * 0.02 * r, hy = y0 + h * 0.27 + o.look[1] * 0.02 * r;
  ctx.fillStyle = C.white || '#fff';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx, hy, w * 0.26, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.7, y0 + h * 0.68, w * 0.11, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (mode === 'wide') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x0 + w * 0.68, y0 + h * 0.3, w * 0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore();
  if (lid < 1) {
    // the eyelid's edge, sagging a little
    ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0 - 0.04 * r, cut); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x0 + w / 2, cut + 0.05 * r, x0 + w + 0.04 * r, cut); ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function brow(ctx, k, kind, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  const ex = k * E.dx * r, by = (E.dy - E.h / 2 - 0.11) * r;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.045 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (kind === 'furrow') { ctx.moveTo(ex + k * 0.16 * r, by - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(ex - k * 0.1 * r, by + 0.04 * r); }
  else { ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, by - 0.07 * r, ex + 0.12 * r, by + 0.02 * r); }
  ctx.stroke();
}

function tears(ctx, phase, C, r) {
  const E = G0.eye;
  const col = C.tear || SKY;
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const x0 = k * (E.dx + 0.02) * r, y0 = (E.dy + E.h / 2 - 0.04) * r, y1 = 0.9 * r;
    const pts = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) {
      const f = i / 6;
      pts.push([x0 + k * 0.05 * r * f + Math.sin(phase * TAU + i * 1.3 + k) * 0.022 * r * f, lerp(y0, y1, f)]);
    }
    const stroke = (w, c) => {
      ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.lineWidth = w; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
      for (const p of pts.slice(1)) ctx.lineTo(p[0], p[1]);
      ctx.stroke();
    };
    stroke(0.115 * r, rgba(C.line, 0.55));
    stroke(0.085 * r, col);
    stroke(0.03 * r, rgba('#ffffff', 0.7));
    // a drop flicking off to the side
    for (const off of [0, 0.5]) {
      const ph = (phase * 1.6 + off + (k > 0 ? 0.25 : 0)) % 1;
      const dx = k * (0.22 + 0.5 * ph) * r, dy = (0.08 - 0.28 * Math.sin(Math.PI * ph) + 0.55 * ph * ph) * r;
      const s = (1 - 0.45 * ph) * r;
      ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(C.line, 0.5); ctx.lineWidth = 0.015 * r;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(x0 + dx, y0 + dy, 0.045 * s, 0.06 * s, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

function mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r) {
  const my = G0.mouthY * r;
  const mo = clamp(o.mouthOpen);
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 0.042 * r; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  ctx.fillStyle = C.mouth;
  switch (o.mouth) {
    case 'o': {
      const rx = (0.055 + 0.11 * mo) * r, ry = (0.04 + 0.16 * mo) * r, cy = my + 0.03 * r * mo;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      if (mo > 0.55) {
        ctx.save(); ctx.clip();
        ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.25);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy + ry * 0.75, rx * 0.7, ry * 0.42, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    }
    case 'smile':
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, my + 0.1 * r, 0.12 * r, my - 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    case 'grin': {
      const d = (0.1 + 0.14 * mo) * r;
      const path = () => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.bezierCurveTo(0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.16 * r, my + d * 0.9, -0.17 * r, my - 0.05 * r); ctx.closePath(); };
      path(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.save(); path(); ctx.clip();
      ctx.fillStyle = mix(ROSE, C.mouth, 0.2);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my + d * 0.7, 0.11 * r, 0.07 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r; path(); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    }
    case 'press':
      // straining: a flat, slightly crimped line
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.11 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(-0.03 * r, my - 0.012 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.03 * r, my + 0.008 * r); ctx.lineTo(0.11 * r, my - 0.004 * r); ctx.stroke();
      break;
    case 'none':
      break;
    default:
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.08 * r, my); ctx.lineTo(0.08 * r, my); ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function pacifier(ctx, a, C, r) {
  const my = G0.mouthY * r + 0.02 * r;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(a);
  // the shield over the mouth, a button, and the ring hanging from it
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.032 * r; ctx.strokeStyle = C.line;
  ctx.fillStyle = C.paci || '#7fcbc0';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, my, 0.2 * r, 0.12 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.35);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-0.07 * r, my - 0.045 * r, 0.07 * r, 0.03 * r, -0.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = mix(C.paci || '#7fcbc0', C.line, 0.25);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, my, 0.045 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.07 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, my + 0.13 * r, 0.085 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.paciRing || '#f6efe2'; ctx.lineWidth = 0.035 * r;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, my + 0.13 * r, 0.085 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}

export function drawBaby(ctx, opts) {
  const o = { ...BABY_DEFAULTS, ...opts, free: { ...BABY_DEFAULTS.free, ...(opts.free || {}) } };
  const { r, C } = o;
  const B = G0.body;
  const lw = r * 0.034;
  const s = o.phoneArm === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
  const fs = -s;
  const clay = o.blush > 0 ? mix(C.skin, '#e2574a', 0.1 * clamp(o.blush)) : C.skin;
  const clayDk = C.skinShade, clayLt = C.skinLight || mix(C.skin, '#ffffff', 0.35);
  const line = C.line, cloth = C.cloth, clothShade = C.clothShade;
  const arm = G0.arm;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(o.x, o.y);
  ctx.rotate(o.tilt);
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';

  // ---- behind the body: the towel cape, the curl, the legs ---------------------------------
  if (o.outfit === 'towel') {
    ctx.fillStyle = C.towel || '#f2f2f2';
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(line, 0.8); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.8;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-1.0 * r, -0.85 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.95 * r, 0.6 * r, -2.05 * r, 2.3 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-1.0 * r, 2.05 * r, 0, 2.35 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.0 * r, 2.05 * r, 2.05 * r, 2.3 * r);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(1.95 * r, 0.6 * r, 1.0 * r, -0.85 * r);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(C.towel || '#f2f2f2', line, 0.3), 0.6); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.6;
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(k * 1.3 * r, 0.2 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(k * 1.62 * r, 1.2 * r, k * 1.6 * r, 2.1 * r); ctx.stroke(); }
  }
  // a single curl on top, sprouting from behind the top edge
  {
    const path = () => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-0.04 * r, -0.92 * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(-0.08 * r, -1.22 * r, 0.12 * r, -1.42 * r, 0.27 * r, -1.3 * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(0.38 * r, -1.2 * r, 0.27 * r, -1.06 * r, 0.16 * r, -1.13 * r);
    };
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = 0.13 * r; ctx.stroke();
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = clay; ctx.lineWidth = 0.13 * r - 2 * lw; ctx.stroke();
  }
  if (o.outfit !== 'sack') {
    ctx.fillStyle = clayDk; ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.9;
    for (const lx of G0.legs) {
      rr(ctx, (lx - G0.legW / 2) * r, (B.b - 0.12) * r, G0.legW * r, (G0.legH + 0.12) * r, 0.08 * r);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }

  // ---- the body -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.fillStyle = clay;
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.save();
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.clip();
  // flat clay shading: a light patch up and left, a darker band low on the body
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clayLt, 0.5);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-0.55 * r, -0.6 * r, 0.52 * r, 0.22 * r, -0.12, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (o.outfit === 'naked' || o.outfit === 'towel') {
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clayDk, 0.55);
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, 1.08 * r, 4 * r, 2 * r);
  }
  if (o.flash > 0) {
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(-0.1 * r, -0.05 * r, 0, -0.1 * r, -0.05 * r, 1.25 * r);
    g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffffff', 0.5 * o.flash));
    g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffffff', 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, -2 * r, 4 * r, 4 * r);
  }
  if (o.outfit === 'onesie') {
    // the onesie: a cloth band across the lower body, two snaps
    const y = G0.band * r;
    ctx.fillStyle = cloth;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, y - 0.05 * r, 2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.lineTo(2 * r, 3 * r); ctx.lineTo(-2 * r, 3 * r); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clothShade, 0.7);
    ctx.fillRect(-2 * r, (B.b - 0.13) * r, 4 * r, r);
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.9;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, y - 0.05 * r, 2 * r, y + 0.02 * r); ctx.stroke();
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
      ctx.fillStyle = mix(cloth, '#9aa3ad', 0.45);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(k * 0.2 * r, (B.b - 0.24) * r, 0.055 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55; ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(k * 0.2 * r - 0.015 * r, (B.b - 0.255) * r, 0.018 * r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  if (o.stain) {
    const st = C.stain || '#e9dfc8';
    const blobs = [[-0.42, 0.98, 0.17], [-0.12, 1.12, 0.12], [0.24, 0.96, 0.1], [-0.25, 1.33, 0.14], [0.5, 1.18, 0.08], [0.05, 0.82, 0.07]];
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(st, 0.95);
    for (const [sx, sy, sr] of blobs) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sx * r, sy * r, sr * r * 1.25, sr * r, 0.4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(st, line, 0.35), 0.55); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.55;
    for (const [sx, sy, sr] of blobs.slice(0, 4)) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sx * r, sy * r, sr * r * 1.25, sr * r, 0.4, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); }
    // a drip running down from the mouth
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(st, 0.95);
    rr(ctx, 0.02 * r, (G0.mouthY + 0.05) * r, 0.07 * r, 0.32 * r, 0.035 * r); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  bodyPath(ctx, r);
  ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw;
  ctx.stroke();

  // ---- the sleep sack: a soft bag over the lower body and the legs, zipped up the middle ---
  if (o.outfit === 'sack') {
    const S = G0.sack;
    const sackPath = () => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.t - 0.07) * r, S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo((S.w + 0.12) * r, (S.t + 0.5) * r, (S.w + 0.1) * r, (S.b - 0.35) * r, (S.w - 0.25) * r, S.b * r);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.b + 0.08) * r, -(S.w - 0.25) * r, S.b * r);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(-(S.w + 0.1) * r, (S.b - 0.35) * r, -(S.w + 0.12) * r, (S.t + 0.5) * r, -S.w * r * 0.97, S.t * r);
      ctx.closePath();
    };
    sackPath(); ctx.fillStyle = cloth; ctx.fill();
    ctx.save(); sackPath(); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(clothShade, 0.75);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(1.55 * r, 1.5 * r, 0.5 * r, 1.2 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    // the binding along the top hem
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(clothShade, 1); ctx.lineWidth = 0.09 * r;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-S.w * r, (S.t + 0.04) * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, (S.t - 0.03) * r, S.w * r, (S.t + 0.04) * r); ctx.stroke();
    // zipper: a seam with teeth and the pull tab at the top
    ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(clothShade, line, 0.35), 0.95); ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.75;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0.06 * r, (S.t + 0.06) * r); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0.1 * r, (S.t + 0.7) * r, 0.04 * r, (S.b + 0.1) * r); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.45;
    for (let i = 1; i < 11; i++) {
      const f = i / 11, zy = lerp(S.t + 0.08, S.b, f) * r, zx = (0.06 + 0.04 * Math.sin(Math.PI * f)) * r;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(zx - 0.03 * r, zy); ctx.lineTo(zx + 0.03 * r, zy); ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    sackPath(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clothShade, '#ffffff', 0.25);
    rr(ctx, 0.0, (S.t + 0.06) * r, 0.12 * r, 0.2 * r, 0.04 * r); ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.6; ctx.stroke();
  }

  // ---- the phone nub: stretched to the bottom corner, the phone out of frame -------------------
  const root = (k) => [k * arm.x * r, arm.y * r];
  nub(ctx, root(s), [s * 2.05 * r, 3.6 * r], arm.t * r, arm.t * 1.35 * r, clay, line, lw);

  // ---- the face -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // blush: always a little (a baby), more on demand
  const bl = 0.32 + 0.68 * clamp(o.blush);
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const bx = k * G0.cheek.dx * r, by = G0.cheek.dy * r;
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(C.blush || ROSE, 0.55 * bl);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx, by, 0.17 * r, 0.09 * r, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    if (o.blush > 0.6) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(C.blush || ROSE, line, 0.35), 0.8); ctx.lineWidth = 0.022 * r;
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx + (i - 1.4) * 0.075 * r, by + 0.045 * r); ctx.lineTo(bx + (i - 0.9) * 0.075 * r, by - 0.045 * r); ctx.stroke(); }
    }
  }
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) eyeAt(ctx, k, o, C, r);
  const brows = o.brows || (o.mouth === 'press' ? 'furrow' : null);
  if (brows) for (const k of [-1, 1]) brow(ctx, k, brows, C, r);
  if (o.tears > 0) tears(ctx, o.tears, C, r);
  if (!(o.pacifier >= 0.5)) mouthAt(ctx, o, C, r);
  if (o.pacifier > 0) pacifier(ctx, o.pacifier, C, r);

  // ---- the free nub ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const f = o.free;
  const t = arm.t * r;
  if (f.mode === 'down') {
    nub(ctx, root(fs), [fs * 1.62 * r, 0.82 * r], t, t, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'wave' || f.mode === 'up') {
    const a = f.angle ?? 0;
    const lift = f.mode === 'wave' ? 0.95 + 0.4 * Math.sin(a) : 1.2 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a);
    const len = 0.62 * r;
    nub(ctx, root(fs), [fs * (arm.x * r + Math.cos(lift) * len), arm.y * r - Math.sin(lift) * len], t, t * 1.05, clay, line, lw);
  } else if (f.mode === 'point') {
    // the nub coming at the lens: it grows, its flat end toward us
    const k = clamp(f.reach ?? 1);
    const hx = lerp(fs * 1.5, fs * 0.3, k) * r, hy = lerp(0.9, 1.0, k) * r, hr = lerp(0.2, 0.7, k) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(fs), [hx, hy], t, hr * 1.6, clay, line, lw);
    rr(ctx, hx - hr, hy - hr * 0.9, 2 * hr, 1.8 * hr, hr * 0.45);
    ctx.fillStyle = mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.08); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = line; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.stroke();
  } else if (f.mode === 'hold') {
    // the nub reaches the point the scene gives (head-relative units); the scene draws the prop
    const hx = (f.at?.[0] ?? fs * 0.6) * r, hy = (f.at?.[1] ?? 1.2) * r;
    nub(ctx, root(fs), [hx, hy], t, t * 1.15, clay, line, lw);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/** Colour set for the baby from a graded palette (G) or the flash palette. */
export function babyColors(G, outfit = 'onesie') {
  return {
    skin: G.skin, skinShade: G.skinShade, skinLight: G.skinLight, hair: G.hair, ink: G.ink, line: G.line, mouth: G.mouth, white: G.white,
    cloth: outfit === 'sack' ? G.sack : G.onesie, clothShade: outfit === 'sack' ? G.sackShade : G.onesieShade,
    towel: G.towel, stain: G.stain, blush: G.blush, tear: G.tear, paci: G.paci, paciRing: G.paciRing,
  };
}

// Props and rooms, drawn in camera space (1080x1920, the phone's front camera). Rooms
// paint well past the frame edges so a tilted or shaken camera never shows a border.

import { clamp, lerp, TAU, hash, ease } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix, rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';

export const CW = 1080, CH = 1920;
const BIG = { x: -900, y: -900, w: CW + 1800, h: CH + 1800 };

export function fillAll(ctx, color) {
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.fillRect(BIG.x, BIG.y, BIG.w, BIG.h);
}

/** Wall with a soft light falloff (brighter toward the window side). */
  ctx.restore();
  return { x, y, w: gw, h };
}

/** Light spill on the floor/wall from an open door at night. */
export function spill(ctx, x, y, w, h, color, a = 0.25) {
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x + w / 2, y + h, 20, x + w / 2, y + h, h * 1.1);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(color, a));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(color, 0));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(x - h, y - h * 0.2, w + 2 * h, h * 1.6);
}

/**
 * A parent as a silhouette (night) or a soft figure. pose: 'stand' | 'sit' | 'slump'.
 * (x, y) = top of head; s = head radius. `eyes`: 'tired' | 'closed' | null.
 */
export function parent(ctx, { x, y, s = 60, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = '#c9ccd1', lean = 0, mouth = null, skin = null }) {
  ctx.save();
  if (mouth === 'o') {
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000000', 0.55);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, hy + 0.5 * s, 0.16 * s, 0.22 * s, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/** Ceiling fan seen from below. angle in radians; blur 0..1 adds ghost blades. */

python3 - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path("/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/props.js"); s = p.read_text()
a = s.index('/**\n * A parent as a silhouette')
b = s.index('/** Ceiling fan seen from below.')
new = r'''/**
 * Adult Clawd (the parent): the classic proportions, a 10u x 6u clay block on four legs with
 * two arm nubs and tall slit eyes. (x, y) = the ground point between the feet; u = unit.
 *   pose 'stand' | 'sit' (on the floor, legs out toward us) | 'slump' (in a chair, tipped back)
 *   eyes 'open' | 'tired' (heavy lids) | 'closed' | null (a pure silhouette)
 *   mouth 'o' (asleep, breathing: breath 0..1) | 'line' | null
 * color fills everything when there is no `clay` (a night silhouette); rim = a rim-light colour.
 */
export function adultClawd(ctx, { x, y, u, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', clay = null, clayDk = null, ink = '#1e1412', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = null, lean = 0, mouth = null, breath = 0.5, arms = [0.25, 0.25] }) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(lean);
  const fill = clay || color, dk = clayDk || (clay ? mix(clay, '#000000', 0.25) : color);
  const lw = clay ? Math.max(2, u * 0.16) : 0;
  const stroke = () => { if (lw) { ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.stroke(); } };
  const legH = pose === 'stand' ? 2.2 : 0;
  const top = -(6 + legH) * u, bot = -legH * u;
  // arm nubs (behind the body), raise angles in radians
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const a = arms[k < 0 ? 0 : 1];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(k * 4.9 * u, top + 3.5 * u);
    ctx.rotate(k < 0 ? a : -a);
    rrect(ctx, k < 0 ? -2.2 * u : 0, -0.5 * u, 2.2 * u, u, 0.2 * u);
    ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // legs: standing under the body; sitting, the feet point at us below the front edge
  ctx.fillStyle = dk;
  for (const lx of [-4, -2, 1, 3]) {
    if (pose === 'stand') { rrect(ctx, lx * u, -2.4 * u, u, 2.4 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
    else { rrect(ctx, (lx - 0.1) * u, bot - 0.5 * u, 1.2 * u, 1.1 * u, 0.3 * u); ctx.fill(); stroke(); }
  }
  // body
  rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u);
  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
  if (clay) {
    ctx.save(); rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u); ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(dk, 0.5); ctx.fillRect(-5 * u, top + 4.2 * u, 10 * u, 2 * u);
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba(mix(clay, '#ffffff', 0.35), 0.35);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-1.6 * u, top + 1.6 * u, 3.4 * u, 1.3 * u, -0.08, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
    rrect(ctx, -5 * u, top, 10 * u, 6 * u, 0.3 * u); stroke();
  }
  if (rim) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = rim; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.3; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-4.6 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.7 * u, top + 0.1 * u); ctx.lineTo(4.95 * u, top + 2.5 * u); ctx.stroke();
  }
  // face: eyes at (+-2.5u, 2u below the top), mouth 3.7u below the top
  const ec = eyeColor || ink;
  if (eyes) {
    for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
      const ex = k * 2.5 * u, ey = top + 2 * u;
      ctx.fillStyle = ec; ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      if (eyes === 'closed') {
        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.32;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ex, ey + 0.8 * u, ex + 0.8 * u, ey + 0.2 * u); ctx.stroke();
      } else if (eyes === 'tired') {
        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey + 0.05 * u, u, 0.95 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
        ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.24;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ex - 0.85 * u, ey); ctx.lineTo(ex + 0.85 * u, ey + k * 0.12 * u); ctx.stroke();
      } else {
        rrect(ctx, ex - 0.5 * u, ey - u, u, 2 * u, 0.15 * u); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  }
  if (mouth === 'o') {
    const o = 0.75 + 0.25 * breath;
    ctx.fillStyle = clay ? '#4a1f2a' : rgba('#000000', 0.55);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, top + 3.9 * u, 0.6 * u, 0.85 * u * o, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    if (lw) { ctx.lineWidth = lw * 0.8; ctx.strokeStyle = ink; ctx.stroke(); }
  } else if (mouth === 'line') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ec; ctx.lineWidth = u * 0.25;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.lineTo(0.7 * u, top + 3.8 * u); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/**
 * The parent (an adult Clawd) as a night silhouette. pose: 'stand' (in the doorway) | 'sit'
 * (on the floor by the crib). (x, y) = the top of the old figure's head and s its head radius,
 * so the figure keeps its place: it stands on the line y + 11.5s and is about 5.5s wide.
 */
export function parent(ctx, { x, y, s = 60, pose = 'stand', color = '#14151b', rim = null, eyes = null, eyeColor = '#c9ccd1', lean = 0, mouth = null }) {
  if (pose === 'stand') {
    adultClawd(ctx, { x, y: y + 11.5 * s, u: 0.56 * s, pose: 'stand', color, rim, eyes, eyeColor, lean, mouth, arms: [-0.5, -0.5] });
  } else {
    // on the floor, slumped against the crib, the feet out toward us
    adultClawd(ctx, { x: x + 0.7 * s, y: y + 6.6 * s, u: 0.62 * s, pose: 'sit', color, rim, eyes, eyeColor, lean, mouth, arms: [-0.9, -0.7] });
  }
}

function sleeper(ctx, t) {
  // armchair: back, seat, and the two arms
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chair; rrect(ctx, 40, 560, 1000, 1500, 170); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chairShade; rrect(ctx, 130, 640, 820, 900, 130); ctx.fill();

  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  // chair arms over the nubs' ends
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chair; rrect(ctx, -10, 1300, 200, 760, 90); ctx.fill(); rrect(ctx, 890, 1300, 200, 760, 90); ctx.fill();

  ctx.strokeStyle = K.blanketShade; ctx.lineWidth = 12;
  for (const x of [330, 560, 790]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 1500); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + 30, 1800, x - 10, 2150); ctx.stroke(); }
}

R=/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video; sed -n "$(grep -n 'export function doorway' $R/props.js | cut -d: -f1),+26p" $R/props.js; grep -n "waterSurface\|1.5 \* SELFIE.r\|1.6 \* SELFIE.r" $R/scenes/bath.js

export function doorway(ctx, G, { x = 690, y = 120, w = 300, h = 760, open = 1, hall = '#ffcf8a', hallAlpha = 1 } = {}) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = G.trim;
  ctx.fillRect(x - 24, y - 24, w + 48, h + 24);
  const gw = w * clamp(open);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + gw, 0);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(hall, 0.95 * hallAlpha));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(mix(hall, '#ffffff', 0.3), 0.85 * hallAlpha));
  ctx.fillStyle = G.doorDark;
  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(x, y, gw, h);
  // the door leaf, swung open toward the room
  ctx.fillStyle = G.door;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + gw, y);
  ctx.lineTo(x + w + 10, y - 20);
  ctx.lineTo(x + w + 10, y + h + 30);
  ctx.lineTo(x + gw, y + h);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  return { x, y, w: gw, h };
}

# 2AM (And I'm Crying): the video

Treatment v3, "the baby's selfie video" (`../TREATMENT.md`), over the v3 lyric sheet
(`../suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`). Every frame is a pure function of song time, painted on a
canvas in headless Chrome and encoded with ffmpeg. The engine is song-agnostic and lives in
`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby, props, the caption
and status-bar styling, the time-of-day grade and the edit rules.

Review surface: `sheets/plates.png` (one cell per clip, 9:16) and `sheets/layout-16x9.png`.

from pathlib import Path
p = Path("/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/README.md"); s = p.read_text()

('''`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby, props, the caption

'''`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby (baby Clawd), props,
the caption and status-bar styling, the time-of-day grade and the edit rules.'''),
('''  `loudness`, `source` (`"synth (...)"`, `"plan"`, or a take with its file in parentheses: render.mjs and the
  preview pick the audio from it),''',
'''  `loudness`, `source` (`"synth (...)"`, `"plan"`, or a take with its file in parentheses; the preview
  plays that file, render.mjs finds the synth wav itself and otherwise needs `--audio`),'''),
('''## How it is put together
''',
'''## The character: baby Clawd

The lead is a baby Clawd, the Claude Code mascot (the protagonist of the "I'm Upping My P(doom)"
video), drawn fresh in Canvas 2D in the flat style. Adult Clawd is a 10 x 6 unit clay block on
four legs with two arm nubs and tall slit eyes; the baby is the same idea made younger:

- body: chubbier and nearly square (2.5r wide, 2.6r tall, r = the scene's `r`), rounder corners,
  the sides bowing out a little; terracotta clay `#d77757` with a light patch up and left, dark
  ink outline; a single curl on top; four short stubby legs in a darker clay.
- face (around `(o.x, o.y)`, where the old round head's centre was, so props still land): eyes
  are bigger, lower, rounded rectangles (0.31r x 0.46r at +-0.44r) with a big highlight dot;
  small mouth at +0.47r; blush always a little, more on demand (hatched above 0.6).
- the phone nub always stretches from the body's side to the bottom corner, widening toward the
  lens (the phone itself is never seen). The free nub: `down`, `wave`, `up`, `hold` (to a point
  the scene gives, where the scene draws the prop), `point`, `none`.
- outfits: `sack` (night: a soft bag over the lower body and the legs, zipped up the middle),
  `onesie` (day: a cloth band across the lower body with two snaps), `naked` (bare clay with
  Clawd's darker band, after the bath), `towel` (a cape behind).
- expressions on the same API as before: eyes `open` / `half` / continuous `open` (a heavy lid
  that droops toward the outer corner) / `closed` (a soft arc) / `squeeze` (> <) / `wide` / `focus`
  (a narrow slit); mouth `line` / `o` (the crying O, with a tongue when wide open) / `smile` /
  `grin` / `press` (a flat crimped line, implies a furrowed brow) / `none`; `tears` (thick streams
  and flicking drops, the value is the animation phase), `blush`, `flash`, `stain` (milk on the
  onesie and a dribble), `look` (eyes and highlights shift), `brows`, and the new `pacifier`
  (0..1: a shield and ring over the mouth; on for 5am quiet, 7:45 "sleeping", 8pm-12am sleeping,
  the 11am and 3pm naps).
- `BABY_GEOM` exports the geometry (the 5am / 1am glowing eyes in the dark use it).
- the parent is an adult Clawd (`props.adultClawd`): a backlit silhouette down the lit hall at
  3am, slumped by the crib at 4am (dim clay, tired eyes over the baby's shoulder), asleep in the
  armchair on the rear camera at 10pm (eyes shut, mouth open, z's); the 12pm spoon comes in on an
  adult nub; the 7:45 IR monitor cut-in shows baby Clawd in its sack.

Palette keys (`tod.js` `BASE`, re-lit by `grade()`, and the night `FLASH` / intro `SCREEN` sets):
`skin` (clay), `skinShade`, `skinLight`, `line`, `ink`, `mouth`, `blush`, `tear`, `paci`,
`paciRing`, `parentSkin`, `parentShade`.

## How it is put together
'''),
('''- `baby.js`, `props.js`, `monitor.js` (the v1 IR monitor view, cut into 7:45 "sleeping").''',

BashKill my orphaned Chromes and harden render.mjs worker cleanup6:49 AM
input
description Kill my orphaned Chromes and harden render.mjs worker cleanup
kill 86567 86569 86571 86573 2>/dev/null; python3 - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path("/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs"); s = p.read_text()
old = '''  const workers = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));'''
new = '''  // launch all workers; if any fails to start, close the ones that did (no orphaned Chromes)
  const launched = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));
  const bad = launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'rejected');
  if (bad.length) {
    await Promise.all(launched.filter((x) => x.status === 'fulfilled').map((x) => closeWorker(x.value)));
    server.closeAllConnections?.(); server.close();
    throw bad[0].reason;
  }
  const workers = launched.map((x) => x.value);'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old = '''    // close each browser, but never hang on it: kill whatever has not exited after 5 s
    await Promise.all(workers.map(async (x) => {
      const proc = x.browser.process();
      await Promise.race([x.browser.close().catch(() => {}), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000))]);
      if (proc && proc.exitCode == null) proc.kill('SIGKILL');
    }));'''
new = '''    await Promise.all(workers.map(closeWorker));'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old = '''async function main() {'''
new = '''/** Close a worker's browser, but never hang on it: kill whatever has not exited after 5 s. */
async function closeWorker(x) {
  const proc = x.browser.process();
  await Promise.race([x.browser.close().catch(() => {}), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000))]);
  if (proc && proc.exitCode == null) proc.kill('SIGKILL');
}

async function main() {'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
# a page that fails to become ready: close its own browser too
old = '''  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${songDir}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 120000 });
  await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 120000 });'''
new = '''  try {
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${songDir}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 120000 });
    await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 120000 });
  } catch (e) {
    await closeWorker({ browser });
    throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page not ready: ${e.message}${errors.length ? '\\n  ' + errors.join('\\n  ') : ''}`);
  }'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
p.write_text(s)
EOF
